diff --git a/assets/bank-import.js b/assets/bank-import.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..3b6328e

@@ -0,0 +1,145 @@
+/* NevaIB bank import staging — window.NB.bankImport. Needs window.NB only
+   (bankSchema when present); no store, no DOM — the Upload page (author.html)
+   owns the preview UI and storage, this file owns the staging rules so they can
+   be unit-tested (tests/js/bank-import.test.js).
+
+   The paste path on the Upload page no longer saves straight away: pasted JSON
+   is staged here first, shown as a preview the student can edit (stem, choices,
+   answer, markscheme, difficulty) or trim (remove items), and only "Import"
+   writes the bank. The staged bank is plain in-memory data — a reload drops it,
+   which is fine: she re-pastes.
+
+   finalize() produces EXACTLY the stored-bank shape the file-upload path builds
+   in author.html processContent (Object.assign(data, {name, uploaded}) +
+   label/subjectLabel defaults), so staged imports and file uploads are
+   indistinguishable to the quiz engine. */
+(function () {
+  "use strict";
+  const NB = window.NB = window.NB || {};
+  if (NB.bankImport) return;
+
+  // above this, the preview renders summary rows instead of per-item editors —
+  // a 20k-item pasted bank must not build 20k textareas
+  const MAX_EDIT_ITEMS = 200;
+
+  const validator = () => (NB.bankSchema && NB.bankSchema.validateQuizBank) || null;
+
+  const slug = (s) => String(s || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "").slice(0, 40);
+
+  // "ai-<topic>.json", "-2"/"-3"… on collision with the stored banks' names
+  function suggestName(data, existingNames) {
+    const names = new Set(existingNames || []);
+    const base = "ai-" + (slug(data && data.topic) || "practice-set");
+    let name = base + ".json", n = 2;
+    while (names.has(name)) name = base + "-" + (n++) + ".json";
+    return name;
+  }
+
+  function parse(text) {
+    try { return { data: JSON.parse(text) }; }
+    catch (e) { return { error: "invalid JSON — " + e.message }; }
+  }
+
+  // stage(text, existingNames) → {ok, staged:{data, name}} | {error}
+  // The AI is told to output the exact bank shape; anything that fails
+  // bankSchema.validateQuizBank never reaches the preview.
+  function stage(text, existingNames) {
+    const p = parse(text);
+    if (p.error) return { error: p.error };
+    const data = p.data;
+    if (!data || typeof data !== "object" || Array.isArray(data) || !Array.isArray(data.items))
+      return { error: "no 'items' array found — is this a quiz bank? (flashcard decks go through the file upload)" };
+    const v = validator();
+    if (!v) return { error: "the page is stale — reload it so the bank validator (assets/bank-schema.js) loads, then try again" };
+    const err = v(data);
+    if (err) return { error: err };
+    return { ok: true, staged: { data: data, name: suggestName(data, existingNames) } };
+  }
+
+  // re-run the shared validator over the staged bank (after every edit/remove)
+  function validate(staged) {
+    const v = validator();
+    if (!v || !staged || !staged.data) return "the page is stale — reload it so the bank validator (assets/bank-schema.js) loads, then try again";
+    return v(staged.data); // null when valid
+  }
+
+  // validate a candidate bank (data with `items` swapped in) without touching staged
+  function validateCandidate(staged, items) {
+    const v = validator();
+    if (!v) return "the page is stale — reload it so the bank validator (assets/bank-schema.js) loads, then try again";
+    return v(Object.assign({}, staged.data, { items: items })); // null when valid
+  }
+
+  // patch = {stem?, choices?, answer?, note?, marks?, markscheme?, tactic?, difficulty?, topic?}
+  // Applied to a COPY of items[index]; the whole bank is re-validated and only a
+  // valid result is committed, so a rejected edit leaves staged data unchanged.
+  // For mcq, empty strings in `choices` are dropped and `answer` must land inside
+  // the surviving choices — the same rules the item editor applies on save.
+  function editItem(staged, index, patch) {
+    if (!staged || !staged.data || !Array.isArray(staged.data.items)) return { error: "nothing staged" };
+    const item = staged.data.items[index];
+    if (!item) return { error: "no such item" };
+    const p = patch || {};
+    const cand = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(item));
+    if (p.stem !== undefined) cand.stem = String(p.stem).trim();
+    if (p.topic !== undefined) { const t = String(p.topic).trim(); if (t) cand.topic = t; else delete cand.topic; }
+    if (p.difficulty !== undefined) {
+      if (NB.bankSchema && NB.bankSchema.DIFFICULTIES.indexOf(p.difficulty) >= 0) cand.difficulty = p.difficulty;
+      else delete cand.difficulty;
+    }
+    if (cand.format === "mcq") {
+      if (p.choices !== undefined) {
+        const kept = (Array.isArray(p.choices) ? p.choices : []).map(c => String(c == null ? "" : c).trim()).filter(c => c !== "");
+        cand.choices = kept;
+      }
+      if (p.answer !== undefined) {
+        const a = parseInt(p.answer, 10);
+        if (!isNaN(a)) cand.answer = a;
+        else if (p.answer === "" || p.answer === null) delete cand.answer;
+      }
+      if (p.note !== undefined) { const n = String(p.note).trim(); if (n) cand.note = n; else delete cand.note; }
+    } else if (cand.format === "written") {
+      if (p.marks !== undefined) {
+        const m = parseInt(p.marks, 10);
+        if (!isNaN(m) && m >= 1) cand.marks = m; else delete cand.marks;
+      }
+      if (p.markscheme !== undefined) {
+        const lines = String(p.markscheme).split("\n").map(l => l.trim()).filter(l => l !== "");
+        if (lines.length) cand.markscheme = lines; else delete cand.markscheme;
+      }
+      if (p.tactic !== undefined) { const t = String(p.tactic).trim(); if (t) cand.tactic = t; else delete cand.tactic; }
+    }
+    const items = staged.data.items.slice();
+    items[index] = cand;
+    const err = validateCandidate(staged, items);
+    if (err) return { error: err };
+    staged.data.items[index] = cand;
+    return { ok: true };
+  }
+
+  function removeItem(staged, index) {
+    if (!staged || !staged.data || !Array.isArray(staged.data.items)) return { error: "nothing staged" };
+    if (!staged.data.items[index]) return { error: "no such item" };
+    const items = staged.data.items.slice();
+    items.splice(index, 1);
+    const err = validateCandidate(staged, items);
+    if (err) return { error: err };
+    staged.data.items.splice(index, 1);
+    return { ok: true };
+  }
+
+  // → {ok, entry} | {error}. The entry mirrors author.html's processContent
+  // storage shape exactly (name/uploaded/label/subjectLabel defaults included).
+  function finalize(staged) {
+    if (!staged || !staged.data) return { error: "nothing staged" };
+    const err = validate(staged);
+    if (err) return { error: err };
+    const data = staged.data;
+    const entry = Object.assign({}, data, { name: String(staged.name || "").trim() || suggestName(data, []), uploaded: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) });
+    if (!entry.label) entry.label = data.topicLabel || entry.name;
+    if (!entry.subjectLabel) entry.subjectLabel = data.subject;
+    return { ok: true, entry: entry };
+  }
+
+  NB.bankImport = { MAX_EDIT_ITEMS, stage, validate, editItem, removeItem, finalize, suggestName };
+})();
diff --git a/assets/bank-schema.js b/assets/bank-schema.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..f7e2d26
--- /dev/null
+++ b/assets/bank-schema.js
@@ -0,0 +1,93 @@
+/* NevaIB content-bank schema — window.NB.bankSchema.
+   Shared validators + vocabulary for practice-question banks and flashcard
+   decks, used by the Upload page (author.html) on upload and on every item edit.
+   The same rules run in tools/validate_content.py over library/ content, so a
+   bank the Upload page accepts cannot drift from what a permanent library file
+   must look like.
+
+   - DIFFICULTIES: the selectable per-item difficulty values. Absent difficulty
+     is "unrated" — every pre-feature item stays valid (zero migration).
+   - DIFFICULTY_ORDER: the ordinal scale the quiz picker's difficulty filter
+     uses; unrated sits below easy.
+   - validateQuizBank(bank) / validateDeck(deck, existingIds): return null when
+     valid, else a short human-readable error string (exact Upload-page wording).
+   - nextSetName(existingNames, topicKey): the next free "Practice set N" for a
+     topic (pure; see the comment on the function).
+
+   Needs window (for NB) and nothing else — no store, no DOM. */
+(function () {
+  "use strict";
+  const NB = window.NB = window.NB || {};
+  if (NB.bankSchema) return;
+
+  const DIFFICULTIES = ["easy", "standard", "hard"];
+  const DIFFICULTY_ORDER = ["unrated", "easy", "standard", "hard"];
+
+  // Optional practice-set metadata: absent fields stay valid (old banks never
+  // carried them), present fields must have the right shape. Mirrors
+  // tools/validate_content.py, which enforces the same rules over library/.
+  function validateSetMeta(d) {
+    if (d.set !== undefined && (typeof d.set !== "string" || !d.set.trim())) return "'set' must be a non-empty string";
+    if (d.setOrder !== undefined && !Number.isInteger(d.setOrder)) return "'setOrder' must be an integer";
+    if (d.codes !== undefined && (!Array.isArray(d.codes) || !d.codes.every(c => typeof c === "string"))) return "'codes' must be an array of strings";
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  function validateQuizBank(bank) {
+    if (typeof bank.subject !== "string" || !bank.subject.trim()) return "missing 'subject' (e.g. chemistry-hl)";
+    const metaErr = validateSetMeta(bank);
+    if (metaErr) return metaErr;
+    if (!Array.isArray(bank.items) || bank.items.length === 0) return "missing or empty 'items' array";
+    const ids = new Set();
+    for (const it of bank.items) {
+      if (!it.id) return "an item is missing 'id'";
+      if (ids.has(it.id)) return "duplicate id '" + it.id + "' (ids must be unique within the file)";
+      ids.add(it.id);
+      if (!it.stem) return "item '" + it.id + "': missing 'stem'";
+      if (it.format === "mcq") {
+        if (!Array.isArray(it.choices) || it.choices.length < 2) return "item '" + it.id + "': needs at least 2 choices";
+        if (!Number.isInteger(it.answer) || it.answer < 0 || it.answer >= it.choices.length) return "item '" + it.id + "': 'answer' must be the 0-based index of the correct choice";
+      } else if (it.format === "written") {
+        if (!it.marks || !Array.isArray(it.markscheme) || !it.markscheme.length) return "item '" + it.id + "': written items need 'marks' and a markscheme array";
+      } else return "item '" + it.id + "': format must be 'mcq' or 'written'";
+      if (it.difficulty !== undefined && DIFFICULTIES.indexOf(it.difficulty) < 0) {
+        return "item '" + it.id + "': difficulty must be 'easy', 'standard' or 'hard'";
+      }
+    }
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  function validateDeck(deck, existingIds) {
+    if (typeof deck.subject !== "string" || !deck.subject.trim()) return "missing 'subject' (e.g. biology-hl)";
+    const metaErr = validateSetMeta(deck);
+    if (metaErr) return metaErr;
+    if (typeof deck.topic !== "string" || !deck.topic.trim()) return "missing 'topic' (must match the quiz bank's topic key)";
+    if (!Array.isArray(deck.cards) || deck.cards.length === 0) return "missing or empty 'cards' array";
+    for (const c of deck.cards) {
+      if (!c.id) return "a card is missing 'id'";
+      if (existingIds.has(c.id)) return "card id '" + c.id + "' already exists in another deck — make ids globally unique (e.g. prefix them)";
+      if (!c.front || !c.back) return "card '" + c.id + "': 'front' and 'back' are both required";
+      if (c.type && c.type !== "qa" && c.type !== "term") return "card '" + c.id + "': type must be 'qa' or 'term'";
+      existingIds.add(c.id);
+    }
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  /* The next free "Practice set N" for a topic. Numbering never goes backwards:
+     N is the smallest free number at or above the lowest "Practice set N" already
+     in use — gaps in the numbering are filled before a new number is appended,
+     and a number below an existing set is never suggested (a topic holding
+     "Practice set 2" gets 3, not the technically-free 1). When no numbered name
+     exists yet the count starts at 1; other names only block their exact match.
+     `topicKey` is carried for callers' messages/suggestions and unused here. */
+  function nextSetName(existingNames, topicKey) {
+    const names = (Array.isArray(existingNames) ? existingNames : []).map(n => String(n));
+    const used = new Set(names);
+    const nums = names.map(n => /^Practice set (\d+)$/.exec(n)).filter(Boolean).map(m => parseInt(m[1], 10));
+    let n = nums.length ? Math.min.apply(null, nums) : 1;
+    while (used.has("Practice set " + n)) n++;
+    return "Practice set " + n;
+  }
+
+  NB.bankSchema = { DIFFICULTIES, DIFFICULTY_ORDER, validateQuizBank, validateDeck, nextSetName };
+})();

+  // Topic dropdown: decks already pool per subject::topic in groups(), so the
+  // options carry real card counts (CARDS is post retired/delete/edit filtering)
+  // plus the deck set names when the decks of a topic carry one. Values stay
+  // "subject::topic" — buildQueue is untouched. Topics order by setOrder only

   function fillTopics() {
     const subj = $("fc-subject").value;
     const topic = $("fc-topic");
+    const prev = topic.value;

-    DECKS.filter(d => subj === "*" || d.subject === subj).forEach(d => {
-      const o = document.createElement("option"); o.value = d.subject + "::" + d.topic; o.textContent = d.subjectLabel + " \u2014 " + d.topicLabel; topic.appendChild(o);
+    const gs = groups().filter(g => subj === "*" || g.subject === subj);
+    const groupOrder = (g) => {
+      const decks = DECKS.filter(d => d.subject === g.subject && d.topic === g.topic);
+      return decks.length && decks.every(d => d.setOrder != null) ? decks[0].setOrder : null;
+    };
+    const ordered = gs.every(g => groupOrder(g) != null) ? gs.slice().sort((a, b) => groupOrder(a) - groupOrder(b)) : gs;
+    ordered.forEach(g => {
+      const decks = DECKS.filter(d => d.subject === g.subject && d.topic === g.topic);
+      const sets = [...new Set(decks.map(d => d.set).filter(Boolean))];
+      let suffix = "";
+      if (sets.length === 1) suffix = " — " + sets[0];
+      else if (sets.length > 1) suffix = " (sets: " + sets.join(", ") + ")";
+      const o = document.createElement("option");
+      o.value = g.subject + "::" + g.topic;
+      o.textContent = g.subjectLabel + " — " + g.topicLabel + " · " + g.total + " cards" + suffix;
+      topic.appendChild(o);
     });
+    // keep the current selection across re-renders (subject change → new list → all)
+    topic.value = (prev && ordered.some(g => g.subject + "::" + g.topic === prev)) ? prev : "*";
   }

diff --git a/assets/mistakes.js b/assets/mistakes.js

index 0000000..2edd961

@@ -0,0 +1,252 @@
+/* NevaIB mistake log — window.NB.mistakes. Needs assets/store.js (+ assets/
+   profile.js for subject hiding, like the other app pages).
+
+   Every wrong answer in a Quiz session is stored as a "q-errors" record by
+   quiz-engine.js; this page is the browsable log over those records. Phase 2
+   optional fields (written by the quiz engine / this page, never required):
+     errorType?  "careless" | "misread" | "content" | "time" | "other" ("" = untagged)
+     reflection? ≤500 chars
+     retries?    [{ts, correct, conf?}] — capped at 20 by the quiz engine
+     retired? / retiredReason? / retiredAt? — reversible soft-hide ("not relevant");
+                 restore removes the three fields and bumps ts; records are never
+                 hard-deleted here or in the sync/backup merge.
+   The page's own filter state lives in "mistakes-filters" (LOCAL_ONLY in
+   assets/store.js — device-local, never synced, scoped per user). */
+(function () {
+  "use strict";
+  const NB = window.NB = window.NB || {};
+  if (NB.mistakes) return;
+  const KEY = "q-errors";
+  const FKEY = "mistakes-filters";
+  const S = NB.store;
+  const esc = (window.NB && window.NB.escapeHtml) || ((s) => String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;").replace(/'/g, "&#39;"));
+
+  const ERROR_TYPES = [["careless", "Careless slip"], ["misread", "Misread the question"], ["content", "Didn't know the content"], ["time", "Ran out of time"], ["other", "Other"]];
+  const STATUSES = [["open", "Unresolved"], ["recovered", "Recovered"], ["retired", "Retired"], ["all", "All"]];
+  const typeLabel = (v) => (ERROR_TYPES.find(t => t[0] === v) || [v, ""])[1];
+  const subjectLabel = (id) => {
+    if (!id) return "";
+    const reg = (NB.SUBJECTS || []).find(s => s.id === id || (NB.canonicalSubject && NB.canonicalSubject(id)) === s.id);
+    return reg ? reg.label : id;
+  };
+
+  // ---------- storage ----------
+  const errors = () => { const v = S.get(KEY, []); return Array.isArray(v) ? v : []; };
+  const find = (errs, key) => errs.find(e => e && e.key === key);
+
+  // "retired" (soft-hidden) beats "recovered" (answered right in a review) beats "open"
+  function statusOf(e) { return e && e.retired ? "retired" : (e && e.resolved ? "recovered" : "open"); }
+
+  function filterRecords(records, f) {
+    f = f || {};
+    const status = f.status || "open";
+    const subject = f.subject || "";
+    const q = String(f.q || "").trim().toLowerCase();
+    return (records || []).filter(e => {
+      if (!e || !e.key) return false;
+      if (status !== "all" && statusOf(e) !== status) return false;
+      if (subject && e.subject !== subject) return false;
+      // per-user subject profile (assets/profile.js), like today.js: a missed
+      // question from a subject the account doesn't take is not shown
+      if (e.subject && NB.profile && NB.profile.hides(e.subject)) return false;
+      if (q && (String(e.stem || "") + " " + String(e.topicLabel || "")).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) < 0) return false;
+      return true;
+    });
+  }
+
+  // open first, then recovered, then retired; most recently touched first within a status
+  function sortRecords(list) {
+    const rank = { open: 0, recovered: 1, retired: 2 };
+    const at = (e) => String((e.lastAttempt > e.lastMissed ? e.lastAttempt : e.lastMissed) || "");
+    return list.slice().sort((a, b) => (rank[statusOf(a)] - rank[statusOf(b)]) || (at(b) < at(a) ? -1 : at(b) > at(a) ? 1 : (b.ts || 0) - (a.ts || 0)));
+  }
+
+  // ---------- mutators (all go through S.set("q-errors", …) → synced + dirty-marked) ----------
+  function retire(key, reason) {
+    const errs = errors(); const e = find(errs, key);
+    if (!e) return false;
+    e.retired = true;
+    e.retiredReason = String(reason || "").trim().slice(0, 200);
+    e.retiredAt = Date.now();
+    e.ts = Date.now();
+    return S.set(KEY, errs) !== false;
+  }
+  function restore(key) {
+    const errs = errors(); const e = find(errs, key);
+    if (!e) return false;
+    delete e.retired; delete e.retiredReason; delete e.retiredAt;
+    e.ts = Date.now();
+    return S.set(KEY, errs) !== false;
+  }
+  function saveReflection(key, text) {
+    const errs = errors(); const e = find(errs, key);
+    if (!e) return false;
+    const t = String(text == null ? "" : text).trim().slice(0, 500);
+    if (t) e.reflection = t; else delete e.reflection;
+    e.ts = Date.now();
+    return S.set(KEY, errs) !== false;
+  }
+  function saveErrorType(key, v) {
+    const errs = errors(); const e = find(errs, key);
+    if (!e) return false;
+    if (ERROR_TYPES.some(t => t[0] === v)) e.errorType = v; else delete e.errorType;
+    e.ts = Date.now();
+    return S.set(KEY, errs) !== false;
+  }
+
+  // ---------- filter state (device-local, "mistakes-filters") ----------
+  function loadFilters() {
+    const f = S.get(FKEY, null);
+    const d = { status: "open", subject: "", q: "" };
+    if (f && typeof f === "object" && !Array.isArray(f)) {
+      if (STATUSES.some(s => s[0] === f.status)) d.status = f.status;
+      if (typeof f.subject === "string") d.subject = f.subject;
+      if (typeof f.q === "string") d.q = f.q;
+    }
+    return d;
+  }
+  const saveFilters = (f) => { S.set(FKEY, f); };
+
+  // ---------- formatting helpers ----------
+  const fmtDay = (iso) => {
+    if (!iso || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(iso)) return "";
+    const [y, m, d] = iso.split("-").map(Number);
+    return new Date(y, m - 1, d).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { day: "numeric", month: "short" });
+  };
+  const fmtTs = (ts) => { const d = new Date(ts); return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? "" : d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { day: "numeric", month: "short" }); };
+  const lastRetry = (e) => { const r = (Array.isArray(e.retries) ? e.retries : []).filter(x => x && typeof x === "object"); return r.length ? r[r.length - 1] : null; };
+  const excerpt = (s, n) => { s = String(s || ""); return s.length > n ? s.slice(0, n).trimEnd() + "…" : s; };
+
+  // ---------- rendering (browser only) ----------
+  const STATUS_LABEL = { open: "Open", recovered: "Recovered", retired: "Retired" };
+
+  function rowHtml(e) {
+    const st = statusOf(e);
+    const retries = lastRetry(e);
+    const missed = [fmtDay(e.firstMissed), fmtDay(e.lastMissed)].filter(Boolean);
+    const missedTxt = missed.length === 2 && missed[0] !== missed[1] ? `Missed ${missed[0]} \u2192 ${missed[1]}` : (missed.length ? `Missed ${missed[missed.length - 1]}` : "");
+    const attempts = e.attempts || 1;
+    const retryTxt = retries ? `last retry ${retries.correct ? "correct" : "wrong"} ${fmtTs(retries.ts)}` : "";
+    const typeTxt = typeLabel(e.errorType);
+    const notes = (e.subject && e.topic)
+      ? `<a class="btn" href="notes.html?subject=${encodeURIComponent(e.subject)}&topic=${encodeURIComponent(e.topic)}">Open topic notes</a>` : "";
+    return `<div class="mk-row" data-key="${esc(e.key)}">
+      <div class="mk-head">
+        <span class="mk-badge mk-${st}">${STATUS_LABEL[st]}</span>
+        ${e.topicLabel ? `<span class="mk-topic">${esc(String(e.topicLabel))}</span>` : ""}
+        ${typeTxt ? `<span class="chip mk-type">${esc(typeTxt)}</span>` : ""}
+      </div>
+      <div class="mk-stem">${esc(excerpt(e.stem, 120))}</div>
+      <div class="mk-meta muted">${[missedTxt, `${attempts} attempt${attempts === 1 ? "" : "s"}`, retryTxt].filter(Boolean).map(esc).join(" \u00b7 ")}</div>
+      ${e.reflection ? `<div class="mk-reflect">${esc(String(e.reflection))}</div>` : ""}
+      ${st === "retired" ? `<div class="mk-why muted">Not relevant${e.retiredReason ? ": " + esc(String(e.retiredReason)) : ""}.</div>` : ""}
+      <div class="mk-actions">
+        <a class="btn mk-retry" href="quiz.html?errkeys=${encodeURIComponent(e.key)}">Retry this question</a>
+        ${notes}
+        <button class="btn mk-edit">Edit</button>
+        ${st === "retired"
+          ? `<button class="btn mk-restore">Restore</button>`
+          : `<button class="btn mk-retire">Not relevant\u2026</button>`}
+      </div>
+      <div class="mk-editor" hidden>
+        <label>Error type
+          <select class="mk-type-sel"><option value="">—</option>${ERROR_TYPES.map(t => `<option value="${t[0]}"${e.errorType === t[0] ? " selected" : ""}>${esc(t[1])}</option>`).join("")}</select>
+        </label>
+        <label>Reflection <span class="muted">(what to do differently, \u2264 500 characters)</span>
+          <textarea class="mk-reflect-in" maxlength="500" rows="3">${esc(e.reflection || "")}</textarea>
+        </label>
+        <button class="btn primary mk-save">Save</button>
+      </div>
+    </div>`;
+  }
+
+  function wireRow(el, row, rerender) {
+    const key = row.getAttribute("data-key");
+    const editor = row.querySelector(".mk-editor");
+    const editBtn = row.querySelector(".mk-edit");
+    if (editBtn) editBtn.addEventListener("click", () => { editor.hidden = !editor.hidden; });
+    const retireBtn = row.querySelector(".mk-retire");
+    if (retireBtn) retireBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
+      const reason = typeof prompt === "function" ? prompt("Why is this one not relevant? (optional)") : "";
+      if (reason === null) return;
+      retire(key, reason); rerender();
+    });
+    const restoreBtn = row.querySelector(".mk-restore");
+    if (restoreBtn) restoreBtn.addEventListener("click", () => { restore(key); rerender(); });
+    const saveBtn = row.querySelector(".mk-save");
+    if (saveBtn) saveBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
+      const sel = row.querySelector(".mk-type-sel"), txt = row.querySelector(".mk-reflect-in");
+      saveErrorType(key, sel ? sel.value : "");
+      saveReflection(key, txt ? txt.value : "");
+      rerender();
+    });
+  }
+
+  function renderList(el, f, rerender) {
+    const box = el.querySelector("#mk-list");
+    const all = errors();
+    const rows = sortRecords(filterRecords(all, f));
+    const EMPTY = {
+      open: "No unresolved mistakes \u2014 nice.",
+      recovered: "Nothing recovered yet \u2014 answer a missed question correctly in a review and it lands here.",
+      retired: "Nothing is marked as not relevant.",
+      all: "No mistakes recorded yet \u2014 they appear automatically when a Quiz answer is wrong."
+    };
+    box.innerHTML = rows.length
+      ? rows.map(rowHtml).join("")
+      : `<p class="muted">${EMPTY[f.status || "open"] || EMPTY.open}</p>`;
+    box.querySelectorAll(".mk-row").forEach(row => wireRow(el, row, rerender));
+  }
+
+  function renderFilters(el, f, rerender) {
+    const subjects = [];
+    errors().forEach(e => { if (e && e.subject && subjects.indexOf(e.subject) < 0) subjects.push(e.subject); });
+    if (f.subject && subjects.indexOf(f.subject) < 0) subjects.push(f.subject);
+    const sel = el.querySelector("#mk-status"), sub = el.querySelector("#mk-subject"), q = el.querySelector("#mk-q");
+    sel.innerHTML = STATUSES.map(s => `<option value="${s[0]}"${f.status === s[0] ? " selected" : ""}>${esc(s[1])}</option>`).join("");
+    sub.innerHTML = `<option value="">All subjects</option>` +
+      subjects.map(id => `<option value="${esc(id)}"${f.subject === id ? " selected" : ""}>${esc(subjectLabel(id))}</option>`).join("");
+    q.value = f.q;
+    const apply = () => {
+      f.status = sel.value; f.subject = sub.value; f.q = q.value;
+      saveFilters(f); rerender();
+    };
+    sel.addEventListener("change", apply);
+    sub.addEventListener("change", apply);
+    q.addEventListener("input", apply);
+  }
+
+  // mounted state: the panel element + live filters, so a store change can re-render
+  let PANEL = null, FILTERS = null;
+
+  function mount(el) {
+    el.innerHTML = `
+      <p class="muted">Every wrong answer in a Quiz session is kept here until a review answers it correctly. Tag why you missed it, write a short reflection, retry a single question, and file the ones that no longer matter as \u201cnot relevant\u201d \u2014 nothing is ever deleted, and retired questions can come back with Restore.</p>
+      <div class="mk-filters">
+        <label>Status <select id="mk-status"></select></label>
+        <label>Subject <select id="mk-subject"></select></label>
+        <label class="mk-q-label">Search <input id="mk-q" type="search" placeholder="stem or topic\u2026"></label>
+      </div>
+      <div id="mk-list"></div>`;
+    PANEL = el;
+    FILTERS = loadFilters();
+    const redraw = () => renderList(el, FILTERS, redraw);
+    renderFilters(el, FILTERS, redraw);
+    redraw();
+  }
+
+  NB.mistakes = { ERROR_TYPES, statusOf, filterRecords, sortRecords, retire, restore, saveReflection, saveErrorType, mount };
+
+  // re-render when q-errors changes (sync arriving, another tab, or this page's
+  // edits) — same debounced watch as today.js; the storage event only fires in
+  // OTHER tabs, where keys are physical ("<user>:q-errors"), hence the suffix match
+  let timer = null;
+  const rerender = () => { if (PANEL && FILTERS) renderList(PANEL, FILTERS, rerender); };
+  const later = () => { clearTimeout(timer); timer = setTimeout(rerender, 300); };
+  if (S && S.onChange) S.onChange((k) => { if (k === KEY) later(); });
+  window.addEventListener("storage", (e) => { if (e && e.key && e.key.indexOf(KEY) >= 0) later(); });
+
+  const boot = () => { const el = document.getElementById("mistakes-panel"); if (el && S) mount(el); };
+  if (document.readyState === "loading") document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", boot);
+  else boot();
+})();

+  // Read-modify-write of the whole "topic-taught" map: Auto deletes the override,
+  // Taught/Not-yet store {taught, t} (the sync merge is newest-t per topic).
+  function setTaughtOverride(topicKey, value) {
+    let map = store.get("topic-taught", {});
+    if (!map || typeof map !== "object" || Array.isArray(map)) map = {};
+    if (value === "auto") delete map[topicKey];
+    else map[topicKey] = { taught: value === "taught", t: Date.now() };
+    store.set("topic-taught", map);
+    fillTopics(); // option suffixes + the panel itself re-render from the new map
+  }
+
+  // ── pure topic model (exported for tests; no DOM, no fetches) ─────────────
+  /* buildTopics(banks) → ordered topic nodes for the picker.
+     banks: seeded entries {file, label, meta?} (meta = the parsed manifest
+     entry; every field on it is optional) and inline uploads
+     {inline: true, items, label, topicLabel, topic?, set?, setOrder?, codes?}.
+     Node: {topicKey, label, count, difficulties {unrated,easy,standard,hard},
+     codes, sets: [{ref, name, order, count, paperStyle, calculatorFree}]}.
+     - Seeded WITH meta: the pre-aggregated meta.topics[] entries drive the nodes
+       (bank files are never fetched here); the bank itself appears as a set of
+       every topic it contributes to, with the per-topic count.
+     - Seeded WITHOUT meta (offline fallback): the bank is its own topic; its
+       size is unknown → count null (the picker shows "?").
+     - Inline uploads: items are aggregated in memory exactly like bankItems()
+       maps them (effective topic = it.topic || bank.topic || bankId; retired
+       skipped; difficulty = valid value else "unrated").
+     Contributions merge across banks by topicKey; set names within a topic are
+     NOT deduped (dupes are suggested away at upload time). Labels: first
+     non-null wins — inline banks are processed last, so seeded labels win. */
+  function buildTopics(banks) {
+    const byKey = {};
+    const nodes = [];
+    const nodeFor = (topicKey, label) => {
+      let n = byKey[topicKey];
+      if (!n) {
+        n = byKey[topicKey] = { topicKey, label: null, count: 0, countKnown: true, difficulties: { unrated: 0, easy: 0, standard: 0, hard: 0 }, codes: [], sets: [] };
+        nodes.push(n);
+      }
+      if (label && n.label == null) n.label = label;
+      return n;
+    };
+    const bump = (n, count) => { if (count == null) n.countKnown = false; else n.count += count; };
+    const addCodes = (n, codes) => (Array.isArray(codes) ? codes : []).forEach(c => { if (typeof c === "string" && c && n.codes.indexOf(c) < 0) n.codes.push(c); });
+
+    (Array.isArray(banks) ? banks : []).forEach(b => {
+      if (!b) return;
+      if (!b.inline && !b.file) return; // neither a seeded bank (file) nor an upload (inline)
+      const meta = b.meta || null;
+      const topics = !b.inline && meta && Array.isArray(meta.topics) ? meta.topics : null;
+      if (!b.inline) {
+        if (topics && topics.length) {
+          topics.forEach(e => {
+            if (!e || typeof e.topic !== "string" || !e.topic) return;
+            const n = nodeFor(e.topic, e.topicLabel || meta.topicLabel || null);
+            bump(n, typeof e.count === "number" ? e.count : null);
+            Object.keys(n.difficulties).forEach(k => { n.difficulties[k] += (e.difficulty && typeof e.difficulty[k] === "number") ? e.difficulty[k] : 0; });
+            // bank-level codes describe the bank's DECLARED topic — attach them only
+            // to that node (or when the bank has a single effective topic). Otherwise
+            // a multi-topic bank's codes would land on every node and keep per-code
+            // topics "not yet taught" forever; per-code nodes inherit their precise
+            // code from the topic-notes manifest (note-id matching in subjectTopics).
+            if (topics.length === 1 || (meta.topic && e.topic === meta.topic)) addCodes(n, meta.codes);
+            n.sets.push({ ref: b, name: b.set || meta.set || meta.label || b.label, order: b.setOrder != null ? b.setOrder : (meta.setOrder != null ? meta.setOrder : null), count: typeof e.count === "number" ? e.count : null, paperStyle: meta.paperStyle || null, calculatorFree: !!meta.calculatorFree });
+          });
+          return;
+        }
+        // offline fallback: the bank is its own topic; count unknown without
+        // fetching the file (which the picker deliberately never does)
+        const n = nodeFor(bankIdFor(b), b.label || null);
+        bump(n, null);
+        n.sets.push({ ref: b, name: b.label, order: b.setOrder != null ? b.setOrder : null, count: null, paperStyle: null, calculatorFree: false });
+        return;
+      }
+      // inline upload: aggregate its items exactly like bankItems() maps them
+      const bid = bankIdFor(b);
+      const perTopic = {}; // topicKey → {label, count, difficulties}
+      (Array.isArray(b.items) ? b.items : []).forEach(it => {
+        if (!it || it.retired === true) return;
+        const tk = String(it.topic || b.topic || bid);
+        const t = perTopic[tk] = perTopic[tk] || { label: null, count: 0, difficulties: { unrated: 0, easy: 0, standard: 0, hard: 0 } };
+        const tl = it.topicLabel || b.topicLabel || null;
+        if (tl && t.label == null) t.label = tl;
+        t.count++;
+        t.difficulties[DIFFICULTIES.indexOf(it.difficulty) >= 0 ? it.difficulty : "unrated"]++;
+      });
+      Object.keys(perTopic).forEach(tk => {
+        const t = perTopic[tk];
+        const n = nodeFor(tk, t.label);
+        bump(n, t.count);
+        Object.keys(t.difficulties).forEach(k => { n.difficulties[k] += t.difficulties[k]; });
+        // same rule as the meta path: bank codes describe the declared bank topic
+        if (Object.keys(perTopic).length === 1 || tk === b.topic) addCodes(n, b.codes);
+        n.sets.push({ ref: b, name: b.set || b.label || b.topicLabel, order: b.setOrder != null ? b.setOrder : null, count: t.count, paperStyle: null, calculatorFree: false });
+      });
+    });
+
+    const nameCmp = (x, y) => String(x.name || "").localeCompare(String(y.name || ""), undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: "base" });
+    nodes.forEach(n => {
+      if (n.label == null) n.label = n.topicKey; // unlabelled topics still need readable option text
+      n.sets.sort((x, y) => {
+        if (x.order == null && y.order == null) return nameCmp(x, y);
+        if (x.order == null) return 1;  // sets without an order sort last
+        if (y.order == null) return -1;
+        return x.order !== y.order ? x.order - y.order : nameCmp(x, y);
+      });
+      n.count = n.countKnown ? n.count : null; // any unknown contributor → "?" for the whole node
+      delete n.countKnown;
+    });
+    nodes.sort((a, b) => String(a.label).localeCompare(String(b.label), undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: "base" }));
+    return nodes;
+  }
+
+  /* taughtState(topicKey, codes, schedule, subjectId, todayISO, overrides):
+     1. a stored "topic-taught" override with a boolean taught wins → source "you";
+     2. else with non-empty codes AND schedule units for the subject: taught iff
+        EVERY code is covered by at least one unit that has already finished
+        (unit.to <= todayISO) → source "schedule";
+     3. otherwise "unknown" (no codes, no schedule for the subject, malformed
+        input). Never throws — any doubt falls back to unknown. */
+  function taughtState(topicKey, codes, schedule, subjectId, todayISO, overrides) {
+    try {
+      if (overrides && typeof overrides === "object" && !Array.isArray(overrides)) {
+        const ov = overrides[topicKey];
+        if (ov && typeof ov.taught === "boolean") return { state: ov.taught ? "taught" : "not-taught", source: "you" };
+      }
+      const cs = Array.isArray(codes) ? codes.filter(c => typeof c === "string" && c) : [];
+      const units = scheduleUnits(schedule, subjectId);
+      if (cs.length && units.length) {
+        const taught = cs.every(c => units.some(u => u && typeof u.to === "string" && u.to <= todayISO && Array.isArray(u.codes) && u.codes.indexOf(c) >= 0));
+        return { state: taught ? "taught" : "not-taught", source: "schedule" };
+      }
+    } catch (e) { /* malformed input → unknown below */ }
+    return { state: "unknown", source: "none" };
+  }
+
+  // Units of the schedule's entry for subjectId. Accepts the whole schedule
+  // ({subjects: {…}}) or a single subject entry ({units: […]}) directly.
+  // Subject ids compare canonically when profile.js is loaded — in BOTH
+  // directions, since class-schedule.json still keys the old id ("maths-aa-sl")
+  // while callers pass the registry id ("math-aa-sl").
+  function scheduleUnits(schedule, subjectId) {
+    if (!schedule || typeof schedule !== "object") return [];
+    let entry = null;
+    if (schedule.subjects && typeof schedule.subjects === "object" && !Array.isArray(schedule.subjects)) {
+      const canon = (window.NB && window.NB.canonicalSubject) || ((x) => x);
+      const id = subjectId == null ? null : String(subjectId);
+      if (id) {
+        entry = schedule.subjects[id] || schedule.subjects[canon(id)] || null;
+        if (!entry) {
+          const key = Object.keys(schedule.subjects).find(k => canon(k) === canon(id));
+          entry = key ? schedule.subjects[key] : null;
+        }
+      }
+    } else if (Array.isArray(schedule.units)) {
+      entry = schedule;
+    }
+    return entry && Array.isArray(entry.units) ? entry.units : [];
+  }
+
+  // Difficulty-range filter for session items. Items without a valid difficulty
+  // rank as "unrated" (the bottom of DIFFICULTY_ORDER). If min > max the two
+  // selects were crossed over — the bounds are swapped so the filter stays
+  // meaningful instead of silently matching nothing.
+  function difficultyPass(it, min, max) {
+    const lo = Math.max(0, DIFFICULTY_ORDER.indexOf(min));
+    const hi = Math.max(0, DIFFICULTY_ORDER.indexOf(max));
+    const a = Math.min(lo, hi), b = Math.max(lo, hi);
+    const d = DIFFICULTIES.indexOf(it && it.difficulty) >= 0 ? it.difficulty : "unrated";
+    const r = DIFFICULTY_ORDER.indexOf(d);
+    return r >= a && r <= b;
   }

+/* NevaIB resources — window.NB.resources. Needs assets/store.js (+ assets/
+   profile.js for subject hiding, like the other app pages).
+
+   Page-level "My resources": a title, subject, topic, type, link and an optional
+   note, tagged to a syllabus topic. Stored in the SAME synced key the notes page
+   uses for its per-note links ("my-res"), so one list powers both:
+     {id: {note?, subject?, topic?, type?, comment?, label, url, t}}
+   — note = a topic-note id (set by the notes page or the "also show on the topic
+   note" checkbox), the rest are this page's tags. Deletions become
+   {note?/subject?, deleted: true, t} tombstones so they win the per-item sync /
+   backup merge (NB.merge.RECORDS["my-res"], newest t wins); assets/backup.js
+   validates the extended shape. Nothing here is pre-seeded.
+
+   Hard rules: a resource is a LINK only — url must be http(s):// or file://
+   (≤2000 chars, same rule as notes.js safeUrl); file contents are never stored
+   (no base64 PDFs in localStorage); and nothing in "my-res" is ever read by the
+   quiz or card engines — resources can never become quiz content. */
+(function () {
+  "use strict";
+  const NB = window.NB = window.NB || {};
+  if (NB.resources) return;
+  const KEY = "my-res";
+  const S = NB.store;
+  const esc = (window.NB && NB.escapeHtml) || ((s) => String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/&/g, "&amp;").replace(/</g, "&lt;").replace(/>/g, "&gt;").replace(/"/g, "&quot;").replace(/'/g, "&#39;"));
+
+  const TYPES = [["website", "Website"], ["video", "Video"], ["doc", "Document / file"], ["practice", "Practice questions"], ["other", "Other"]];
+  const typeLabel = (v) => (TYPES.find(t => t[0] === v) || [v, ""])[1];
+  const subjectLabel = (id) => {
+    if (!id) return "";
+    const reg = (NB.SUBJECTS || []).find(s => s.id === id || (NB.canonicalSubject && NB.canonicalSubject(id)) === s.id);
+    return reg ? reg.label : id;
+  };
+  const safeUrl = (u) => { const s = String(u || "").trim(); return /^(https?:\/\/|file:\/\/)\S+$/i.test(s) && s.length <= 2000 ? s : ""; };
+  const newId = () => "r" + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6);
+
+  // ---------- storage ----------
+  const all = () => { const v = S.get(KEY, {}); return v && typeof v === "object" && !Array.isArray(v) ? v : {}; };
+
+  // shared validation for add + edit; returns {rec} or {error}. opts.allowNoSubject
+  // lets an edit keep a record that never had a subject (note-scoped links).
+  function validate(input, opts) {
+    const label = String(input.label == null ? "" : input.label).trim().slice(0, 200);
+    if (!label) return { error: "Add a title." };
+    const url = safeUrl(input.url);
+    if (!url) return { error: "Links must start with http://, https:// or file:// (max 2000 characters)." };
+    const subject = NB.canonicalSubject ? NB.canonicalSubject(String(input.subject || "").trim()) : String(input.subject || "").trim();
+    if (!subject && !(opts && opts.allowNoSubject)) return { error: "Pick a subject." };
+    const rec = { label: label, url: url, t: Date.now() };
+    if (subject) rec.subject = subject;
+    const topic = String(input.topic == null ? "" : input.topic).trim().slice(0, 120);
+    if (topic) rec.topic = topic;
+    if (TYPES.some(t => t[0] === input.type)) rec.type = input.type;
+    const comment = String(input.comment == null ? "" : input.comment).trim().slice(0, 500);
+    if (comment) rec.comment = comment;
+    const note = String(input.note == null ? "" : input.note).trim();
+    if (note) rec.note = note.slice(0, 120);
+    return { rec: rec };
+  }
+
+  function add(input) {
+    const v = validate(input);
+    if (v.error) return v;
+    const map = all();
+    const id = newId();
+    map[id] = v.rec;
+    if (S.set(KEY, map) === false) return { error: "Could not save (storage full?)." };
+    return { ok: true, id: id };
+  }
+
+  function update(id, input) {
+    const map = all(), cur = map[id];
+    if (!cur) return { error: "That resource no longer exists." };
+    // the row editor sends no subject — an edit keeps the record's own subject
+    // (and a note-scoped record stays note-scoped: allowNoSubject)
+    const merged = Object.assign({}, input, { subject: input.subject === undefined ? cur.subject : input.subject });
+    const v = validate(merged, { allowNoSubject: !cur.subject && input.subject === undefined });
+    if (v.error) return v;
+    const rec = v.rec;
+    rec.t = Math.max(Date.now(), (cur.t || 0) + 1); // must beat the record this edit replaces
+    rec.note = input.note === undefined ? cur.note : rec.note; // edits keep a note link unless the caller changed it
+    map[id] = rec;
+    if (S.set(KEY, map) === false) return { error: "Could not save (storage full?)." };
+    return { ok: true, id: id };
+  }
+
+  function remove(id) {
+    const map = all(), cur = map[id];
+    if (!cur) return false;
+    // tombstone keeps subject/note for context; deletion must win the merge
+    const t = { deleted: true, t: Math.max(Date.now(), (cur.t || 0) + 1) };
+    if (cur.note) t.note = cur.note;
+    if (cur.subject) t.subject = cur.subject;
+    map[id] = t;
+    return S.set(KEY, map) !== false;
+  }
+
+  // ---------- topic index (for the datalist + the "also show on the note" link) ----------
+  // {subjectId: [{key, code, title, id}]} from library/topic-notes/manifest.json
+  let TOPICS = null;
+  function setTopicIndex(manifest) {
+    const out = {};
+    ((manifest && manifest.notes) || []).forEach((n) => {
+      if (!n || !n.subject || !n.file) return;
+      const key = String(n.file).split("/").pop().replace(/\.json$/i, "");
+      (out[n.subject] = out[n.subject] || []).push({ key: key, code: n.code || "", title: n.title || "", id: n.id || "" });
+    });
+    Object.keys(out).forEach((k) => out[k].sort((a, b) => (a.code < b.code ? -1 : a.code > b.code ? 1 : 0)));
+    TOPICS = out;
+    return out;
+  }
+  function loadTopics() {
+    if (TOPICS) return Promise.resolve(TOPICS);
+    const base = (typeof location !== "undefined" && String(location.pathname).includes("/app/")) ? "../" : "";
+    return fetch(base + "library/topic-notes/manifest.json", { cache: "no-store" })
+      .then((r) => { if (!r.ok) throw new Error(r.status); return r.json(); })
+      .then((m) => setTopicIndex(m))
+      .catch(() => { TOPICS = TOPICS || {}; return TOPICS; });
+  }
+  const topicsFor = (subject) => ((TOPICS || {})[subject] || []);
+  const noteIdFor = (subject, topic) => {
+    const hit = topicsFor(subject).find((t) => t.key === topic);
+    return hit ? hit.id : "";
+  };
+
+  // ---------- filtering / sorting ----------
+  function filterRecords(records, f) {
+    f = f || {};
+    const subject = f.subject || "";
+    const type = f.type || "";
+    const q = String(f.q || "").trim().toLowerCase();
+    return Object.entries(records || {}).filter(([, r]) => {
+      if (!r || r.deleted) return false;
+      if (!r.label || !safeUrl(r.url)) return false;
+      if (subject && r.subject !== subject) return false;
+      if (type && r.type !== type) return false;
+      // per-user subject profile (assets/profile.js), like mistakes.js
+      if (r.subject && NB.profile && NB.profile.hides(r.subject)) return false;
+      if (q && (String(r.label || "") + " " + String(r.topic || "") + " " + String(r.comment || "")).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) < 0) return false;
+      return true;
+    });
+  }
+  const sortRecords = (list) => list.slice().sort((a, b) => (b[1].t || 0) - (a[1].t || 0));
+
+  // deep links a row offers: the topic's notes page and the Upload page's AI picker
+  function notesUrl(rec) {
+    return (rec.subject && rec.topic) ? `notes.html?subject=${encodeURIComponent(rec.subject)}&topic=${encodeURIComponent(rec.topic)}` : "";
+  }
+  function makeQuestionsUrl(rec) {
+    if (!rec.subject) return "";
+    return `author.html?ai-subject=${encodeURIComponent(rec.subject)}${rec.topic ? "&ai-topic=" + encodeURIComponent(rec.topic) : ""}`;
+  }
+
+  // ---------- rendering (browser only) ----------
+  const fmtTs = (ts) => { const d = new Date(ts); return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? "" : d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { day: "numeric", month: "short" }); };
+
+  function topicOptions(subject) {
+    return topicsFor(subject).map((t) => ({ v: t.key, l: (t.code ? t.code + " " : "") + t.title }));
+  }
+
+  function rowHtml(rec, id) {
+    const nu = notesUrl(rec), mu = makeQuestionsUrl(rec);
+    return `<div class="res-row" data-id="${esc(id)}">
+      <div class="res-head">
+        <a class="res-link" href="${esc(safeUrl(rec.url))}" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">${esc(rec.label)}</a>
+        ${rec.type ? `<span class="chip res-type">${esc(typeLabel(rec.type))}</span>` : ""}
+      </div>
+      <div class="res-meta muted">${[
+        subjectLabel(rec.subject) ? esc(subjectLabel(rec.subject)) : "",
+        rec.topic ? esc(rec.topic) : "",
+        fmtTs(rec.t) ? "added " + fmtTs(rec.t) : ""
+      ].filter(Boolean).join(" \u00b7 ")}</div>
+      ${rec.comment ? `<div class="res-comment">${esc(rec.comment)}</div>` : ""}
+      <div class="res-actions">
+        ${nu ? `<a class="btn" href="${esc(nu)}">Topic notes</a>` : ""}
+        ${mu ? `<a class="btn res-makeq" href="${esc(mu)}">Make questions\u2026</a>` : ""}
+        <button type="button" class="btn res-edit">Edit</button>
+        <button type="button" class="btn res-del">Delete</button>
+      </div>
+      <div class="res-editor" hidden>
+        <label>Title <input class="res-e-label" maxlength="200" value="${esc(rec.label)}"></label>
+        <label>Link <input class="res-e-url" maxlength="2000" value="${esc(rec.url)}"></label>
+        <label>Type
+          <select class="res-e-type"><option value="">—</option>${TYPES.map((t) => `<option value="${t[0]}"${rec.type === t[0] ? " selected" : ""}>${esc(t[1])}</option>`).join("")}</select>
+        </label>
+        <label>Topic <span class="muted">(syllabus code or key, e.g. a1-1)</span>
+          <input class="res-e-topic" maxlength="120" value="${esc(rec.topic || "")}">
+        </label>
+        <label>Note <span class="muted">(what it is / why it helped, \u2264 500 characters)</span>
+          <textarea class="res-e-comment" maxlength="500" rows="2">${esc(rec.comment || "")}</textarea>
+        </label>
+        <button type="button" class="btn primary res-save">Save</button>
+      </div>
+    </div>`;
+  }
+
+  function wireRow(panel, row, rerender) {
+    const id = row.getAttribute("data-id");
+    const editor = row.querySelector(".res-editor");
+    const editBtn = row.querySelector(".res-edit");
+    if (editBtn) editBtn.addEventListener("click", () => { editor.hidden = !editor.hidden; });
+    const delBtn = row.querySelector(".res-del");
+    if (delBtn) delBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
+      if (!remove(id)) {
+        const msg = panel.querySelector("#res-msg");
+        if (msg) msg.textContent = "Could not delete (storage full?).";
+      }
+      rerender();
+    });
+    const saveBtn = row.querySelector(".res-save");
+    if (saveBtn) saveBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
+      const val = (c) => { const e = row.querySelector(c); return e ? e.value : ""; };
+      const res = update(id, { label: val(".res-e-label"), url: val(".res-e-url"), type: val(".res-e-type"), topic: val(".res-e-topic"), comment: val(".res-e-comment"), note: undefined });
+      const msg = panel.querySelector("#res-msg");
+      if (res.error) { if (msg) msg.textContent = res.error; return; } // keep her in-progress editor open
+      if (msg) msg.textContent = "";
+      rerender();
+    });
+  }
+
+  function renderList(panel, f, rerender) {
+    const box = panel.querySelector("#res-list");
+    const rows = sortRecords(filterRecords(all(), f));
+    box.innerHTML = rows.length
+      ? rows.map(([id, r]) => rowHtml(r, id)).join("")
+      : `<p class="muted">No resources here yet${(f.subject || f.type || f.q) ? " for this filter" : ""} — add one above. Resources are your own links; they never turn into quiz questions.</p>`;
+    box.querySelectorAll(".res-row").forEach((row) => wireRow(panel, row, rerender));
+    const count = panel.querySelector("#res-count");
+    if (count) count.textContent = rows.length ? `${rows.length} resource${rows.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}` : "";
+  }
+
+  // filter subjects come from the records (a subject with no resources has no row
+  // to filter); the account's profile hides subjects it doesn't take
+  function filterSubjects(f) {
+    const subjects = [];
+    Object.values(all()).forEach((r) => {
+      if (r && !r.deleted && r.subject && subjects.indexOf(r.subject) < 0 && !(NB.profile && NB.profile.hides(r.subject))) subjects.push(r.subject);
+    });
+    if (f && f.subject && subjects.indexOf(f.subject) < 0) subjects.push(f.subject);
+    subjects.sort();
+    return subjects;
+  }
+
+  // options-only refresh (no listener rebinding): a sync can bring in a subject
+  // the dropdown has never seen while the page is open
+  function refreshFilterSubjects(panel, f) {
+    const sel = panel.querySelector("#res-subject");
+    if (!sel) return;
+    sel.innerHTML = `<option value="">All subjects</option>` +
+      filterSubjects(f).map((id) => `<option value="${esc(id)}"${f.subject === id ? " selected" : ""}>${esc(subjectLabel(id))}</option>`).join("");
+  }
+
+  function renderFilters(panel, f, rerender) {
+    const sel = panel.querySelector("#res-subject"), typ = panel.querySelector("#res-type"), q = panel.querySelector("#res-q");
+    sel.innerHTML = `<option value="">All subjects</option>` +
+      filterSubjects(f).map((id) => `<option value="${esc(id)}"${f.subject === id ? " selected" : ""}>${esc(subjectLabel(id))}</option>`).join("");
+    typ.innerHTML = `<option value="">All types</option>` +
+      TYPES.map((t) => `<option value="${t[0]}"${f.type === t[0] ? " selected" : ""}>${esc(t[1])}</option>`).join("");
+    q.value = f.q;
+    const apply = () => { f.subject = sel.value; f.type = typ.value; f.q = q.value; renderList(panel, f, rerender); };
+    sel.addEventListener("change", apply);
+    typ.addEventListener("change", apply);
+    q.addEventListener("input", apply);
+  }
+
+  function renderAddForm(panel, rerender) {
+    const form = panel.querySelector("#res-add-form"), msg = panel.querySelector("#res-msg");
+    const subject = panel.querySelector("#res-subject-add"), topic = panel.querySelector("#res-topic"), list = panel.querySelector("#res-topics");
+    const noteWrap = panel.querySelector("#res-note-wrap"), noteChk = panel.querySelector("#res-note-link");
+    const fillSubjects = () => {
+      const allowed = (NB.SUBJECTS || []).filter((s) => !(NB.profile && NB.profile.hides(s.id)));
+      subject.innerHTML = `<option value=""></option>` +
+        allowed.map((s) => `<option value="${esc(s.id)}">${esc(s.label)}</option>`).join("");
+    };
+    const fillTopics = () => {
+      const opts = topicOptions(subject.value);
+      list.innerHTML = opts.map((t) => `<option value="${esc(t.v)}">${esc(t.l)}</option>`).join("");
+      const has = topicsFor(subject.value).some((t) => t.key === topic.value.trim());
+      noteWrap.hidden = !has;
+      if (has) noteChk.checked = true;
+    };
+    subject.addEventListener("change", fillTopics);
+    topic.addEventListener("input", fillTopics);
+    form.addEventListener("submit", (e) => {
+      e.preventDefault();
+      const note = noteChk.checked ? noteIdFor(subject.value, topic.value.trim()) : "";
+      const res = add({
+        label: panel.querySelector("#res-label").value,
+        subject: subject.value,
+        topic: topic.value,
+        type: panel.querySelector("#res-type-add").value,
+        url: panel.querySelector("#res-url").value,
+        comment: panel.querySelector("#res-comment").value,
+        note: note,
+      });
+      if (res.error) { msg.textContent = res.error; return; }
+      msg.textContent = "";
+      form.reset();
+      fillTopics();
+      rerender();
+    });
+    fillSubjects();
+    loadTopics().then(fillTopics);
+  }
+
+  // mounted state: the panel element + live filters, so a store change can re-render
+  let PANEL = null, FILTERS = null;
+
+  function mount(el) {
+    el.innerHTML = `

Each one is tagged to a subject and (optionally) a syllabus topic, and can also show on that topic's note. +      </div>
+      <form id="res-add-form" class="res-add">
+        <div class="res-grid">

+          <label>Subject <select id="res-subject-add"></select></label>

+        <p><button type="submit" class="btn primary">Add resource</button> <span id="res-msg" class="res-msg" role="status"></span></p>
+      </form>
+      <div id="res-list"></div>`;
+    PANEL = el;
+    FILTERS = { subject: "", type: "", q: "" };
+    const redraw = () => renderList(el, FILTERS, redraw);
+    renderFilters(el, FILTERS, redraw);
+    renderAddForm(el, redraw);
+    redraw();
+  }

+  NB.resources = { TYPES, safeUrl, all, filterRecords, sortRecords, validate, add, update, remove, setTopicIndex, topicsFor, noteIdFor, notesUrl, makeQuestionsUrl, mount };

+  // re-render when my-res changes (sync arriving, another tab, or this page's edits)
+  let timer = null;
+  const rerender = () => { if (PANEL && FILTERS) { refreshFilterSubjects(PANEL, FILTERS); renderList(PANEL, FILTERS, rerender); } };
+  const later = () => { clearTimeout(timer); timer = setTimeout(rerender, 300); };
+  if (S && S.onChange) S.onChange((k) => { if (k === KEY) later(); });

   const fcRank = (s) => [s && s.t || 0, s && s.seen || 0, String(s && s.due || "")];
   const errRank = (e) => [e && e.ts || 0, String(e && (e.lastAttempt > e.lastMissed ? e.lastAttempt : e.lastMissed) || ""), e && e.attempts || 0];
 
+  // Field-aware merge of two q-error records with the same key (Phase 2). `w` is the
+  // errRank winner, `l` the loser. retries are a union of both devices' outcomes
+  // (deduped by ts+correct identity, ascending by ts — the engines cap at 20 on their
+  // next write); reflection/errorType fill in from the loser only when the winner
+  // lacks them; retired/retiredReason/retiredAt ride the winner alone, since ts
+  // already decides retire/restore races. Records without the optional fields are
+  // untouched by all of this.
+  const errMerge = (w, l) => {
+    const out = Object.assign({}, w);
+    const seen = new Set(), retries = [];
+    (Array.isArray(w.retries) ? w.retries : []).concat(Array.isArray(l.retries) ? l.retries : []).forEach(r => {
+      if (!r || typeof r !== "object") return;
+      const id = JSON.stringify([r.ts, r.correct]);
+      if (seen.has(id)) return;
+      seen.add(id); retries.push(r);
+    });
+    if (retries.length) out.retries = retries.sort((x, y) => (x.ts || 0) - (y.ts || 0));
+    ["reflection", "errorType"].forEach(f => { if (out[f] === undefined && l[f] !== undefined) out[f] = l[f]; });
+    return out;
+  };
+

+    const current = brk ? [] : units.filter(u => u && isISO(u.from) && isISO(u.to) && u.from <= date && date <= u.to);
+    const next = units.find(u => u && isISO(u.from) && u.from > date) || null;
     return { current, brk, next };
   }
 
+  // Active units across the profile's subjects: today within [from,to] (the class
+  // schedule is approximate — see class-schedule.json). Returns
+  // [{subject, label, unit}] so suggestions can point at what is being taught now.
+  function activeUnits(schedule, subjects, today) {
+    const out = [];
+    (Array.isArray(subjects) ? subjects : []).forEach(row => {
+      const [s, label] = row || [];
+      unitsFor(schedule, s, today).current.forEach(u => {
+        if (u && isISO(u.from) && isISO(u.to)) out.push({ subject: s, label: label || s, unit: u });
+      });
+    });
+    return out;
+  }
+
+  // Open (unresolved, unretired) missed questions grouped by topic label, most open
+  // first, then the most recently missed. The input for the mistakes suggestion:
+  // each group carries the record keys for a quiz.html?errkeys= retry link.
+  function openMistakes(errors, hides) {
+    const groups = {};
+    (Array.isArray(errors) ? errors : []).forEach(e => {
+      if (!e || e.resolved || e.retired || typeof e.key !== "string" || !e.key) return;
+      if (e.subject && hides && hides(e.subject)) return;
+      const label = [e.topicLabel, e.topic, e.bankId].find(v => typeof v === "string" && v) || "your mistakes";
+      const g = groups[label] = groups[label] || { label, keys: [], count: 0, last: "" };
+      g.count++;
+      g.keys.push(e.key);
+      if (isISO(e.lastMissed) && e.lastMissed > g.last) g.last = e.lastMissed;
+    });
+    return Object.values(groups).sort((a, b) => (b.count - a.count) || (b.last < a.last ? -1 : b.last > a.last ? 1 : 0) || (a.label < b.label ? -1 : 1));
+  }
+
+  // Dated items the panel knows about, normalized to {date, label, href, where}:
+  // the school deadline calendar (NB.deadlines, exported by assets/deadlines.js —
+  // records {d, t, tag, subject?}) and coursework stage dates (tracks[].stages[]
+  // .dates[] in library/coursework/coursework.json). Sorted defensively like
+  // deadlines.js does; entries with a subject the profile hides are dropped, and
+  // records without a real date or label are skipped.
+  function datedItems(deadlines, coursework, hides) {
+    const out = [];
+    (Array.isArray(deadlines) ? deadlines : []).forEach(x => {
+      if (!x || !isISO(x.d) || typeof x.t !== "string" || !x.t) return;
+      if (x.subject && hides && hides(x.subject)) return;
+      out.push({ date: x.d, label: x.t, href: "../deadlines.html", where: "calendar" });
+    });
+    (coursework && Array.isArray(coursework.tracks) ? coursework.tracks : []).forEach(tr => {
+      if (!tr || (tr.subject && hides && hides(tr.subject))) return;
+      (Array.isArray(tr.stages) ? tr.stages : []).forEach(st => {
+        (st && Array.isArray(st.dates) ? st.dates : []).forEach(dv => {
+          if (!dv || !isISO(dv.date)) return;
+          const label = [dv.label, st.title, tr.label].find(v => typeof v === "string" && v);
+          if (!label) return;
+          out.push({ date: dv.date, label: label, href: "coursework.html", where: "planner" });
+        });
+      });
+    });
+    // the deadline calendar and the coursework planner share events (the sciences
+    // IA days) under slightly different labels — dedupe by date + normalized label
+    // (bare date is not enough: distinct events can share one)
+    const seen = new Set();
+    return out.filter(x => {
+      const k = x.date + "|" + labelKey(x.label);
+      if (seen.has(k)) return false;
+      seen.add(k);
+      return true;
+    }).sort((a, b) => (a.date < b.date ? -1 : a.date > b.date ? 1 : 0));
+  }
+
+  // Near-term dated items: today (inclusive) through today + leadDays.
+  function nearTermItems(items, today, leadDays) {
+    const horizon = addDays(today, leadDays);
+    return (Array.isArray(items) ? items : []).filter(x => x && isISO(x.date) && today <= x.date && x.date <= horizon);
+  }
+
+  // Longer-range dated items: past the near-term window, inside the long lead
+  // window — shown as "Start thinking about …", never earlier.
+  function startThinkingItems(items, today, leadDays, longLeadDays) {
+    const nearEnd = addDays(today, leadDays), farEnd = addDays(today, longLeadDays);
+    return (Array.isArray(items) ? items : []).filter(x => x && isISO(x.date) && x.date > nearEnd && x.date <= farEnd);
+  }
+
+  // Up to two suggested next steps, ranked data-driven: due cards first, then open
+  // mistakes, then an active unit closing this week, then imminent deadline prep.
+  // Every suggestion carries a visible reason and a direct link (existing link
+  // formats only); without data behind it, nothing is suggested.
+  function suggestActions(inp) {
+    inp = inp || {};
+    const today = inp.today;
+    if (!isISO(today)) return [];
+    const out = [];
+    const due = inp.due || 0;
+    if (due > 0) {
+      out.push({ title: due + " card" + (due === 1 ? "" : "s") + " due", reason: "your review schedule says so", href: inp.dueHref || "cards.html", link: "Review them" });
+    } else if ((inp.own || 0) > 0) {
+      out.push({ title: inp.own + " of your own cards due", reason: "your review schedule says so", href: "cards.html", link: "Review them" });
+    }
+    const m = openMistakes(inp.errors, inp.hides)[0];
+    if (m) {
+      out.push({
+        title: "Open mistakes on " + clip(m.label, 40),
+        reason: m.count + " missed question" + (m.count === 1 ? "" : "s") + " not yet fixed",
+        href: "quiz.html?errkeys=" + m.keys.slice(0, MISTAKE_KEYS_MAX).map(encodeURIComponent).join(","),
+        link: "Retry them"
+      });
+    }
+    // first closing unit that actually yields a direct link — a closing unit
+    // without a note must not suppress one that has
+    const closings = (Array.isArray(inp.units) ? inp.units : [])
+      .filter(u => u && isISO(u.unit && u.unit.to) && today <= u.unit.to && u.unit.to <= addDays(today, UNIT_CLOSE_DAYS))
+      .sort((a, b) => (a.unit.to < b.unit.to ? -1 : a.unit.to > b.unit.to ? 1 : 0));
+    for (let i = 0; i < closings.length; i++) {
+      const cu = closings[i];
+      const code = (cu.unit && Array.isArray(cu.unit.codes) ? cu.unit.codes : [])[0];
+      const note = cu.subject && code ? ((inp.notes || {})[canon(cu.subject) + "|" + code]) : null;
+      const L = note ? noteLinks(note) : null;
+      const href = L && (L.quiz || L.notes);
+      if (!href) continue;
+      out.push({
+        title: cu.unit.to === today ? "Unit closes today" : "Unit closes this week",
+        reason: clip(cu.unit.unit, 60) + " ends " + fmtDay(cu.unit.to) + " (class schedule, approximate)",
+        href: href, link: L.quiz ? "Quiz the unit" : "Open notes"
+      });
+      break;
+    }
+    const next = (Array.isArray(inp.nearItems) ? inp.nearItems : [])[0];
+    if (next && isISO(next.date)) {
+      out.push({
+        title: "Deadline ahead",
+        reason: clip(next.label, 60) + " — " + fmtDay(next.date) + " (" + relDay(next.date, today) + ")",
+        href: next.href || "../deadlines.html", link: next.where === "planner" ? "Open planner" : "Open calendar"
+      });
+    }
+    return out.slice(0, 2);
+  }

   const fromParam = (id, ids) => "from=" + encodeURIComponent(id + (ids && ids.length ? ":" + ids.join(",") : ""));
   function quizHref(bankId, ids, label, n) {

+        subject: "biology-hl", topic: "c1-1-enzymes-metabolism", topicLabel: "C1.1 — Enzymes & metabolism (Nov unit)",
+        format: "mcq", stem: "Seeded mistake stem", attempts: 1, firstMissed: "2026-11-18", lastMissed: "2026-11-18", resolved: false, ts: 1763450000000 }]
+    };
+    await page.evaluateOnNewDocument(s => { for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(s)) localStorage.setItem(k, JSON.stringify(v)); }, seed);
+    const manifestReqs = [];

+    await page.goto(BASE + "/app/index.html", { waitUntil: "networkidle0" });

+      const b = document.getElementById("today-body");
+      return b && b.textContent.indexOf("Loading today's plan") === -1;
+    }, { timeout: 15000, polling: 100 });

+        .find(x => (x.querySelector("h4") || {}).textContent && /Suggested/.test(x.querySelector("h4").textContent));
+      return b ? [...b.querySelectorAll(".td-subj")].map(d => ({ title: d.querySelector("strong").textContent, href: d.querySelector("a").getAttribute("href") })) : [];
+    });

+    // dated windows pinned by the fake clock, membership derived from the live

+      const iso = d => d.getFullYear() + "-" + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
+      const today = iso(new Date());
+      const add = (s, n) => { const [y, m, dd] = s.split("-").map(Number); const t = new Date(y, m - 1, dd, 12); t.setDate(t.getDate() + n); return iso(t); };
+      const nearEnd = add(today, 21), farEnd = add(today, 60);
+      const cw = await (await fetch("../library/coursework/coursework.json")).json();
+      const items = window.NB.deadlines

+      (Array.isArray(cw.tracks) ? cw.tracks : []).forEach(tr => {
+        if (tr && tr.subject && window.NB.profile.hides(tr.subject)) return;
+        (Array.isArray(tr.stages) ? tr.stages : []).forEach(st => (Array.isArray(st.dates) ? st.dates : []).forEach(dv => {
+          const label = [dv.label, st.title, tr.label].find(v => typeof v === "string" && v);
+          if (dv && label) items.push({ d: dv.date, label });
+        }));
+      });
+      items.sort((a, b) => (a.d < b.d ? -1 : 1));
+      return {
+        near: items.filter(x => today <= x.d && x.d <= nearEnd).map(x => x.label),
+        think: items.filter(x => x.d > nearEnd && x.d <= farEnd).map(x => x.label)
+      };
+    });
+    const coming = await page.evaluate(() => {

+        .find(x => (x.querySelector("h4") || {}).textContent && /Coming up/.test(x.querySelector("h4").textContent));
+      if (!b) return { near: [], think: [] };
+      const uls = [...b.querySelectorAll("ul")];
+      const texts = ul => ul ? [...ul.querySelectorAll("li")].map(li => li.textContent) : [];
+      return { near: texts(uls[0]), think: texts(uls[1]) };
+    });
+    assert.strictEqual(coming.near.length, win.near.length,
+      "near-term list exactly covers [today, today+21] (got " + coming.near.length + ", want " + win.near.length + ")");
+    win.near.forEach((label, i) => assert.ok(coming.near[i].includes(label.slice(0, 70)), "near item in date order: " + label));
+    assert.strictEqual(coming.think.length, win.think.length,
+      "long-lead list exactly covers (today+21, today+60] (got " + coming.think.length + ", want " + win.think.length + ")");

+t("q-errors: same key merges field-aware — retries unioned, deduped by ts+correct, ascending", () => {
+  const local = [{ key: "k1", ts: 300, retries: [{ ts: 1, correct: false }, { ts: 9, correct: false }] }];
+  const server = [{ key: "k1", ts: 200, retries: [{ ts: 1, correct: false }, { ts: 2, correct: true }] }];
+  const e = M("q-errors", local, server).find(x => x.key === "k1");
+  assert.deepStrictEqual(plain(e.retries), [{ ts: 1, correct: false }, { ts: 2, correct: true }, { ts: 9, correct: false }]);
+});
+t("q-errors: winner's reflection/errorType win; missing ones fill from the loser", () => {
+  const local = [{ key: "k1", ts: 300, errorType: "careless" }];
+  const server = [{ key: "k1", ts: 200, errorType: "misread", reflection: "re-read the stem" }];
+  const e = M("q-errors", local, server).find(x => x.key === "k1");
+  assert.strictEqual(e.errorType, "careless", "winner's value wins");
+  assert.strictEqual(e.reflection, "re-read the stem", "loser fills the winner's gap");
+});
+t("q-errors: retries only on the loser are kept; retired fields never fill in from the loser", () => {
+  const local = [{ key: "k1", ts: 300 }];
+  const server = [{ key: "k1", ts: 200, retries: [{ ts: 4, correct: true, conf: "sure" }], retired: true, retiredReason: "off-syllabus", retiredAt: 9 }];
+  const e = M("q-errors", local, server).find(x => x.key === "k1");
+  assert.deepStrictEqual(plain(e.retries), [{ ts: 4, correct: true, conf: "sure" }]);
+  assert.strictEqual(e.retired, undefined);
+  assert.strictEqual(e.retiredReason, undefined);
+  assert.strictEqual(e.retiredAt, undefined);
+});
+t("q-errors: records without the new fields merge exactly as before", () => {

+  const server = [{ key: "k1", attempts: 2, ts: 200, resolved: false }];
+  const e = M("q-errors", local, server).find(x => x.key === "k1");

+// node --test tests/js — the Upload page's staged import (assets/bank-import.js):
+// stage → preview edits → finalize, with bank-schema validation re-run after
+// every change. The entry produced by finalize() must be byte-shape-equal to
+// what the file-upload path stores (author.html processContent).
+"use strict";
+const test = require("node:test");
+const assert = require("node:assert/strict");
+const { makeEnv, FakeStorage, plain } = require("./harness");
+
+const mk = () => makeEnv({ storage: new FakeStorage({}) }).load("bank-schema.js").load("bank-import.js");
+const BI = (e) => e.NB.bankImport;
+
+const J = JSON.stringify;
+const mkBank = () => ({
+  subject: "biology-hl",
+  topic: "a1-1",
+  topicLabel: "A1.1 Water",
+  items: [
+    { id: "p01", format: "mcq", topic: "a1-1", stem: "Why is water polar?", choices: ["O is more electronegative", "H bonds to itself", "It is symmetric"], answer: 0, note: "electronegativity" },
+    { id: "p02", format: "mcq", topic: "a1-1", stem: "A solvent dissolves…", choices: ["polar solutes", "lipids", "nothing"], answer: 0 },
+    { id: "p03", format: "written", topic: "a1-1", marks: 2, stem: "Explain cohesion.", markscheme: ["hydrogen bonding (1)", "between water molecules (1)"], tactic: "name the bond" },
+  ],
+});
+
+test("stage: a valid pasted bank stages with a suggested unique name; junk is refused", () => {
+  const e = mk();
+  const res = BI(e).stage(J(mkBank()), ["ai-a1-1.json"]);
+  assert.ok(res.ok, res.error);
+  assert.equal(res.staged.name, "ai-a1-1-2.json", "collision with the existing name gets -2");
+  assert.equal(res.staged.data.items.length, 3);
+  assert.ok(BI(e).stage("{oops", []).error, "malformed JSON refused");
+  assert.ok(BI(e).stage(J({ cards: [] }), []).error, "a deck is not a quiz bank");
+  assert.ok(BI(e).stage(J({ subject: "biology-hl", items: [{ id: "p01" }, { id: "p01" }] }), []).error, "schema violation (duplicate id) refused");
+});
+
+test("suggestName: slugs the topic, avoids collisions, survives a missing topic", () => {
+  const e = mk();
+  const s = BI(e).suggestName;
+  assert.equal(s({ topic: "enzymes-metabolism" }, []), "ai-enzymes-metabolism.json");
+  assert.equal(s({ topic: "C1.1 Enzymes & metabolism!" }, []), "ai-c1-1-enzymes-metabolism.json");
+  assert.equal(s({ topic: "a1-1" }, ["ai-a1-1.json", "ai-a1-1-2.json"]), "ai-a1-1-3.json");
+  assert.equal(s({}, []), "ai-practice-set.json");
+});
+
+test("editItem: mcq choices are trimmed/empties dropped, answer must land inside them", () => {
+  const e = mk();
+  const bi = BI(e);
+  const staged = bi.stage(J(mkBank()), []).staged;
+  // dropping a choice and leaving answer=2 must fail: two choices survive, index 2 is past the end
+  let r = bi.editItem(staged, 0, { choices: ["", "H bonds to itself", "It is symmetric"], answer: 2 });
+  assert.ok(r.error, "answer out of range after dropping a choice");
+  // a valid re-answer fixes it
+  r = bi.editItem(staged, 0, { choices: ["", "H bonds to itself", "It is symmetric"], answer: 1 });
+  assert.ok(r.ok, r.error);
+  // a committed edit REPLACES the item object (atomic validate-then-commit), so re-read
+  let item = staged.data.items[0];
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(item.choices), ["H bonds to itself", "It is symmetric"]);
+  assert.equal(item.answer, 1);
+  // stem/difficulty edits; an unknown difficulty value removes the field
+  assert.ok(bi.editItem(staged, 0, { stem: "  Why is water polar?  ", difficulty: "hard" }).ok);
+  item = staged.data.items[0];
+  assert.equal(item.stem, "Why is water polar?");
+  assert.equal(item.difficulty, "hard");
+  assert.ok(bi.editItem(staged, 0, { difficulty: "impossible" }).ok);
+  assert.equal(staged.data.items[0].difficulty, undefined);
+  // a bad edit to item 1 surfaces as a bank-level error but staged.data keeps the edit attempt out
+  const snapshot = J(staged.data);
+  r = bi.editItem(staged, 1, { choices: [], answer: 0 });
+  assert.ok(r.error, "removing every choice is a bank error");
+  assert.equal(J(staged.data), snapshot, "the validator's rejection leaves the staged data unchanged in items[1]");
+});
+
+test("editItem: written items — marks must be ≥1, markscheme becomes one point per line", () => {
+  const e = mk();
+  const bi = BI(e);
+  const staged = bi.stage(J(mkBank()), []).staged;
+  const item = () => staged.data.items[2]; // committed edits replace the object — re-read
+  assert.ok(bi.editItem(staged, 2, { marks: "0" }).error, "marks < 1 breaks the schema");
+  assert.ok(bi.editItem(staged, 2, { marks: "3", markscheme: "  pt one (1) \n\npt two (1)\npt three (1)" }).ok);
+  assert.equal(item().marks, 3);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(item().markscheme), ["pt one (1)", "pt two (1)", "pt three (1)"]);
+  assert.ok(bi.editItem(staged, 2, { tactic: " " }).ok, "empty tactic is removed");
+  assert.equal(item().tactic, undefined);
+  const empty = bi.stage(J(mkBank()), []).staged;
+  assert.ok(bi.editItem(empty, 2, { markscheme: "" }).error, "no markscheme at all breaks the schema");
+});
+
+test("removeItem: items can be trimmed; the last one cannot (a bank needs items)", () => {
+  const e = mk();
+  const bi = BI(e);
+  const staged = bi.stage(J(mkBank()), []).staged;
+  assert.ok(bi.removeItem(staged, 1).ok);
+  assert.equal(staged.data.items.length, 2);
+  assert.ok(bi.removeItem(staged, 0).ok);
+  const r = bi.removeItem(staged, 0);
+  assert.ok(r.error, "the last item cannot be removed");
+  assert.equal(staged.data.items.length, 1, "the failed remove left the item in place");
+  assert.ok(bi.removeItem(staged, 99).error, "out-of-range index refused");
+});
+
+test("finalize: entry matches the file-upload storage shape exactly (name/uploaded/label/subjectLabel)", () => {
+  const e = mk();
+  const bi = BI(e);
+  const staged = bi.stage(J(mkBank()), []).staged;
+  staged.name = "ai-a1-1.json";
+  const res = bi.finalize(staged);
+  assert.ok(res.ok, res.error);
+  const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(res.entry), {
+    subject: "biology-hl", topic: "a1-1", topicLabel: "A1.1 Water", items: mkBank().items,
+    name: "ai-a1-1.json", uploaded: today, label: "A1.1 Water", subjectLabel: "biology-hl",
+  }, "label/subjectLabel defaults mirror processContent; original fields preserved");
+  // a bank without topicLabel gets the file name as its label
+  const noLabel = bi.stage(J({ subject: "chemistry-hl", topic: "s1-1", items: mkBank().items }), []).staged;
+  const res2 = bi.finalize(noLabel);
+  assert.equal(res2.entry.label, "ai-s1-1.json");
+  assert.equal(res2.entry.subjectLabel, "chemistry-hl");
+  // a staged bank broken after staging refuses to finalize
+  const broken = bi.stage(J(mkBank()), []).staged;
+  broken.data.items[0].answer = 99;
+  assert.ok(bi.finalize(broken).error, "finalize re-validates");
+});
