+// node --test tests/js — NB.bankSchema (assets/bank-schema.js): the shared bank/deck
+// validators moved out of author.html, the optional practice-set metadata checks
+// (set/setOrder/codes/difficulty) and the nextSetName suggestion helper.
+"use strict";
+const test = require("node:test");
+const assert = require("node:assert/strict");
+const { makeEnv, plain } = require("./harness");
+
+const env = makeEnv({}).load("bank-schema.js");
+const S = env.NB.bankSchema;
+
+const mcq = (id, over) => Object.assign({ id, format: "mcq", stem: "Stem " + id, choices: ["a", "b", "c", "d"], answer: 0 }, over);
+const written = (id, over) => Object.assign({ id, format: "written", stem: "Stem " + id, marks: 2, markscheme: ["point (1)", "point (1)"] }, over);
+const bank = (items, over) => Object.assign({ subject: "chemistry-hl", items }, over);
+
+test("bankSchema publishes the difficulty vocabulary", () => {
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(S.DIFFICULTIES), ["easy", "standard", "hard"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(S.DIFFICULTY_ORDER), ["unrated", "easy", "standard", "hard"]);
+});
+
+test("validateQuizBank: valid banks pass, old messages unchanged", () => {
+  assert.equal(S.validateQuizBank(bank([mcq("a1"), written("w1", { answer: undefined })])), null);
+  assert.equal(S.validateQuizBank(bank([mcq("a1")], { topic: "chem-s1", topicLabel: "Structure 1" })), null, "legacy bank-level fields still fine");
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([mcq("a1")], { subject: "  " })), /missing 'subject'/);
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([])), /missing or empty 'items'/);
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([{ id: "x", format: "mcq", stem: "s", choices: ["a"], answer: 0 }])), /at least 2 choices/);
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([{ id: "x", format: "mcq", stem: "s", choices: ["a", "b"], answer: 5 }])), /0-based index/);
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([{ id: "x", format: "written", stem: "s" }])), /written items need 'marks'/);
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([mcq("a1"), mcq("a1")])), /duplicate id 'a1'/);
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([{ format: "mcq", stem: "s", choices: ["a", "b"], answer: 0 }])), /missing 'id'/);
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([mcq("a1", { stem: "" })])), /item 'a1': missing 'stem'/);
+});
+
+test("validateQuizBank: optional new fields are accepted when well-formed", () => {
+  assert.equal(S.validateQuizBank(bank([mcq("a1", { difficulty: "hard" }), written("w1", { difficulty: "easy" })],
+    { set: "Practice set 1", setOrder: 3, codes: ["S1.1", "2.1"] })), null);
+});
+
+test("validateQuizBank: bad item difficulty is rejected, message names the item and the allowed values", () => {
+  for (const bad of ["bogus", "", "Easy", "unrated", 2, null]) {
+    const err = S.validateQuizBank(bank([mcq("a1"), mcq("a2", { difficulty: bad })]));
+    assert.match(err, /item 'a2': difficulty must be 'easy', 'standard' or 'hard'/, "difficulty " + JSON.stringify(bad));
+  }
+});
+
+test("validateQuizBank: set/setOrder/codes shape checks (absent stays valid, present-but-empty is an error)", () => {
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([mcq("a1")], { set: "" })), /'set' must be a non-empty string/);
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([mcq("a1")], { set: "   " })), /'set' must be a non-empty string/);
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([mcq("a1")], { set: 7 })), /'set' must be a non-empty string/);
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([mcq("a1")], { set: null })), /'set' must be a non-empty string/, "explicit null counts as present (mirrors tools/validate_content.py)");
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([mcq("a1")], { setOrder: "2" })), /'setOrder' must be an integer/);
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([mcq("a1")], { setOrder: 1.5 })), /'setOrder' must be an integer/);
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([mcq("a1")], { setOrder: true })), /'setOrder' must be an integer/);
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([mcq("a1")], { codes: "S1.1" })), /'codes' must be an array of strings/);
+  assert.match(S.validateQuizBank(bank([mcq("a1")], { codes: ["S1.1", 2] })), /'codes' must be an array of strings/);
+  // zero-migration: a bank with none of the new fields (the keys simply absent) stays valid
+  const legacy = { subject: "chemistry-hl", items: [mcq("a1")] };
+  assert.equal("set" in legacy, false);
+  assert.equal(S.validateQuizBank(legacy), null);
+});
+
+test("validateDeck: existing behaviour unchanged, new fields checked", () => {
+  const ids = new Set();
+  assert.equal(S.validateDeck({ subject: "biology-hl", topic: "enzymes", cards: [{ id: "c1", front: "F", back: "B" }, { id: "c2", type: "term", front: "F", back: "B" }] }, ids), null);
+  assert.match(S.validateDeck({ subject: "biology-hl", topic: "enzymes", cards: [] }, new Set()), /missing or empty 'cards'/);
+  assert.match(S.validateDeck({ subject: "biology-hl", cards: [{ id: "c1", front: "F", back: "B" }] }, new Set()), /missing 'topic'/);
+  assert.match(S.validateDeck({ subject: "biology-hl", topic: "t", cards: [{ id: "c1", front: "F" }] }, new Set()), /'front' and 'back' are both required/);
+  assert.match(S.validateDeck({ subject: "biology-hl", topic: "t", cards: [{ id: "c1", front: "F", back: "B", type: "nope" }] }, new Set()), /type must be 'qa' or 'term'/);
+  const dup = new Set(["c1"]);
+  assert.match(S.validateDeck({ subject: "biology-hl", topic: "t", cards: [{ id: "c1", front: "F", back: "B" }] }, dup), /already exists in another deck/);
+  assert.equal(S.validateDeck({ subject: "biology-hl", topic: "t", set: "Practice set 2", setOrder: 2, codes: ["C1.1"], cards: [{ id: "c3", front: "F", back: "B" }] }, new Set()), null);
+  assert.match(S.validateDeck({ subject: "biology-hl", topic: "t", set: 3, cards: [{ id: "c3", front: "F", back: "B" }] }, new Set()), /'set' must be a non-empty string/);
+  assert.match(S.validateDeck({ subject: "biology-hl", topic: "t", setOrder: 1.5, cards: [{ id: "c3", front: "F", back: "B" }] }, new Set()), /'setOrder' must be an integer/);
+  assert.match(S.validateDeck({ subject: "biology-hl", topic: "t", codes: {}, cards: [{ id: "c3", front: "F", back: "B" }] }, new Set()), /'codes' must be an array of strings/);
+});
+
+test("nextSetName: fills the first free number at or above the lowest used number", () => {
+  const next = (names) => S.nextSetName(names, "chem-s1");
+  assert.equal(next([]), "Practice set 1");
+  assert.equal(next(["Practice set 1"]), "Practice set 2");
+  assert.equal(next(["Practice set 1", "Practice set 3"]), "Practice set 2");
+  assert.equal(next(["Practice set 2"]), "Practice set 3");
+  assert.equal(next(["Practice set 3", "Practice set 1", "Practice set 2"]), "Practice set 4");
+  assert.equal(next(["Quiz bank", "My practice set"]), "Practice set 1", "unrelated names never block the numbered sequence");
+  assert.equal(next(["Practice set 1x"]), "Practice set 1", "only an exact name blocks its number");
+  assert.equal(next(["Practice set 2", "My set 1"]), "Practice set 3");
+  assert.equal(S.nextSetName("not-an-array"), "Practice set 1", "malformed input stays pure and safe");
+});
+
+test("bankSchema publishes exactly the documented exports", () => {
+  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(env.NB.bankSchema).sort(), ["DIFFICULTIES", "DIFFICULTY_ORDER", "nextSetName", "validateDeck", "validateQuizBank"]);
+});

+// node --test tests/js — mistake-log page logic (assets/mistakes.js over "q-errors"):
+// status ranking, filters, tag/reflection edits, reversible retire. The store/sync
+// side of q-errors is pinned in store.test.js + auth-merge.test.js; this file pins
+// the page engine the mistake-log page mounts.
+"use strict";
+const test = require("node:test");
+const assert = require("node:assert/strict");
+const { makeEnv, FakeStorage, plain } = require("./harness");
+
+const J = JSON.stringify;
+const mk = (data) => makeEnv({ storage: new FakeStorage(data) }).load("store.js").load("profile.js").load("mistakes.js");
+const M = (e) => e.NB.mistakes;
+
+const err = (over) => Object.assign({
+  key: "bio-molecules-water#wm01", bankId: "bio-molecules-water", id: "wm01",
+  stem: "Why do water molecules attract each other?", attempts: 1, ts: 100,
+  firstMissed: "2026-11-19", lastMissed: "2026-11-19", resolved: false,
+}, over);
+
+test("statusOf: retired beats recovered beats open", () => {
+  const e = mk();
+  assert.equal(M(e).statusOf(err()), "open");
+  assert.equal(M(e).statusOf(err({ resolved: true })), "recovered");
+  assert.equal(M(e).statusOf(err({ resolved: true, retired: true })), "retired");
+  assert.equal(M(e).statusOf(err({ retired: true })), "retired");
+});
+
+test("filterRecords: status filter (retired hidden from open), subject + profile hiding, search", () => {
+  const e = mk({
+    "profile": J({ subjects: ["biology-hl"] }),
+    "q-errors": J([
+      err(), // open bio
+      err({ key: "b#2", id: "2", resolved: true, stem: "Recovered stem" }), // recovered bio
+      err({ key: "b#3", id: "3", retired: true, retiredReason: "not in our book", stem: "Retired stem" }), // retired bio
+      err({ key: "c#4", id: "4", stem: "Chem misread", subject: "chemistry-hl" }), // out-of-profile
+    ]),
+  });
+  const recs = plain(e.NB.store.get("q-errors"));
+  const r = M(e);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(r.filterRecords(recs, { status: "open" }).map(x => x.key)), ["bio-molecules-water#wm01"],
+    "open hides recovered AND retired");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(r.filterRecords(recs, { status: "recovered" }).map(x => x.key)), ["b#2"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(r.filterRecords(recs, { status: "retired" }).map(x => x.key)), ["b#3"]);
+  const all = r.filterRecords(recs, { status: "all" });
+  assert.equal(all.length, 3, "all shows every status but still respects the profile");
+  assert.ok(all.every(x => x.subject !== "chemistry-hl"), "profile hides chemistry");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(r.filterRecords(recs, { status: "all", subject: "chemistry-hl" }).map(x => x.key)), [],
+    "explicit subject filter still respects the profile");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(r.filterRecords(recs, { status: "all", q: "misread" }).map(x => x.key)), [],
+    "search covers the stem — no chemistry hit outside the profile");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(r.filterRecords(recs, { status: "all", q: "attract" }).map(x => x.key)), ["bio-molecules-water#wm01"],
+    "search narrows to the matching stem");
+});
+
+test("retire/restore: reversible soft-hide with a reason, never deleted, ts bumped", () => {
+  const e = mk({ "q-errors": J([err()]) });
+  const r = M(e);
+  assert.equal(r.retire("bio-molecules-water#wm01", "  beyond the syllabus  "), true);
+  let rec = plain(e.NB.store.get("q-errors"))[0];
+  assert.equal(rec.retired, true);
+  assert.equal(rec.retiredReason, "beyond the syllabus", "reason trimmed");
+  assert.equal(rec.retiredAt > 0, true);
+  assert.ok(rec.ts > 100, "ts bumped for the merge");
+  assert.equal(rec.key, "bio-molecules-water#wm01", "record still present — never hard-deleted");
+  assert.equal(r.restore("bio-molecules-water#wm01"), true);
+  rec = plain(e.NB.store.get("q-errors"))[0];
+  assert.equal(rec.retired, undefined);
+  assert.equal(rec.retiredReason, undefined);
+  assert.equal(rec.retiredAt, undefined);
+  assert.equal(r.retire("nope#1", "x"), false, "unknown key refused");
+  assert.equal(r.restore("nope#1"), false);
+});
+
+test("saveReflection/saveErrorType: caps and vocabulary; clear on empty", () => {
+  const e = mk({ "q-errors": J([err()]) });
+  const r = M(e);
+  assert.equal(r.saveReflection("bio-molecules-water#wm01", "  read the units!  "), true);
+  assert.equal(plain(e.NB.store.get("q-errors"))[0].reflection, "read the units!");
+  r.saveReflection("bio-molecules-water#wm01", "x".repeat(600));
+  assert.equal(plain(e.NB.store.get("q-errors"))[0].reflection.length, 500, "reflection capped at 500");
+  r.saveReflection("bio-molecules-water#wm01", "");
+  assert.equal(plain(e.NB.store.get("q-errors"))[0].reflection, undefined, "empty reflection clears");
+  assert.equal(r.saveErrorType("bio-molecules-water#wm01", "misread"), true);
+  assert.equal(plain(e.NB.store.get("q-errors"))[0].errorType, "misread");
+  assert.equal(r.saveErrorType("bio-molecules-water#wm01", "invented"), true);
+  assert.equal(plain(e.NB.store.get("q-errors"))[0].errorType, undefined, "unknown type clears the tag");
+  assert.equal(r.saveReflection("nope#1", "x"), false);
+});
+
+test("sortRecords: open first, then recovered, then retired; most recent miss first within a status", () => {
+  const e = mk();
+  const r = M(e);
+  const list = [
+    err({ key: "b#r", id: "r", resolved: true, lastMissed: "2026-11-19" }),
+    err({ key: "b#t", id: "t", retired: true, lastMissed: "2026-11-19" }),
+    err({ key: "b#o2", id: "o2", lastMissed: "2026-11-18" }),
+    err({ key: "b#o1", id: "o1", lastMissed: "2026-11-19" }),
+  ];
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(r.sortRecords(list).map(x => x.key)), ["b#o1", "b#o2", "b#r", "b#t"]);
+});

+// Page-level resources (no note; subject/topic/type/comment tags) share the key:

+test("backup: tagged page-level resources restore; tombstone without url survives; tagged junk dropped", () => {

+  const good = {
+    subject: "biology-hl", topic: "a1-1", type: "video",

+  };

+  file.keys["my-res"].badJ2 = { subject: "chemistry-hl", label: "js", url: "javascript:alert(1)", t: 22 };
+  file.keys["my-res"].badJ3 = { subject: "", label: "empty subject", url: "https://x", t: 23 };
+  file.keys["my-res"].badJ4 = { note: "bio-a1-1", comment: "x".repeat(501), label: "long comment", url: "https://x", t: 24 };
+  const b = mk2({});
+  const p = b.NB.backup.parse(J(file));
+  assert.equal(p.ok, true, p.errors.join("; "));
+  assert.ok(plain(p.warnings).some(w => /4 unreadable record.*my-res/.test(w)), "junk tagged records reported");
+  assert.equal(b.NB.backup.restore(p, "merge").ok, true);
+  const got = plain(b.NB.store.get("my-res"));
+  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(got).sort(), ["badT1", "p1"], "tagged record + bare tombstone kept, junk dropped");
+  assert.equal(got.p1.comment, "good osmosis bit");
+});

+// node --test tests/js — quiz-engine's topic picker: the pure model
+// (buildTopics), the teaching-status resolver (taughtState) and the
+// difficulty-range filter (difficultyPass). The engine is loaded WITHOUT firing
+// DOMContentLoaded (readyState "loading"), exactly like retired.test.js parks
+// boot() — so no fetches run and only the exported pure functions are exercised.
+"use strict";
+const test = require("node:test");
+const assert = require("node:assert/strict");
+const { makeEnv, plain } = require("./harness");
+
+function engine() {
+  const env = makeEnv({ readyState: "loading" }).load("store.js").load("quiz-engine.js");
+  return { env, QE: env.NB.quizEngine };
+}
+
+// ── fixtures ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+const metaBank = (file, label, topics, over) => Object.assign(
+  { file, label, meta: Object.assign({ file, subject: "chemistry-hl", subjectLabel: "Chemistry HL", label, topics }, over) },
+  {}
+);
+
+const SCHED = { subjects: { "biology-hl": { units: [
+  { from: "2026-08-26", to: "2026-09-25", codes: ["A1.1", "B1.1"] },
+  { from: "2026-11-02", to: "2026-11-27", codes: ["C1.1"] }
+] } } };
+
+// ── buildTopics ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+test("buildTopics: manifest-meta banks share one topic; counts, histograms, codes and labels merge", () => {
+  const { QE } = engine();
+  const a = metaBank("library/questions/chem-p1a-x.json", "Paper 1A — Structure",
+    [
+      { topic: "chem-s1-1", topicLabel: "Structure 1.1 — Moles", count: 5, difficulty: { unrated: 2, easy: 1, standard: 2, hard: 0 } },
+      { topic: "chem-s1-2", topicLabel: "Structure 1.2 — Formulas", count: 3, difficulty: { unrated: 3, easy: 0, standard: 0, hard: 0 } }
+    ],
+    { codes: ["S1.1", "S1.2"], paperStyle: "1A", calculatorFree: true });
+  const b = metaBank("library/questions/chem-p1b-x.json", "Paper 1B — Data",
+    [{ topic: "chem-s1-1", topicLabel: "Structure 1.1 — Moles", count: 2, difficulty: { unrated: 2, easy: 0, standard: 0, hard: 0 } }],
+    { codes: ["S1.1"], paperStyle: "1B" });
+  const nodes = QE.buildTopics([a, b]);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(nodes.map(n => n.topicKey)), ["chem-s1-1", "chem-s1-2"], "sorted by label (both 'Structure 1.x — …')");
+  const n1 = nodes[0];
+  assert.equal(n1.count, 7, "per-topic counts from both banks sum up");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(n1.difficulties), { unrated: 4, easy: 1, standard: 2, hard: 0 });
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(n1.codes), ["S1.1"], "multi-topic bank a's codes attach to no node (no declared topic — no leak); single-topic bank b's codes do");
+  assert.equal(n1.label, "Structure 1.1 — Moles");
+  assert.equal(n1.sets.length, 2, "each bank is one set of the shared topic");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(n1.sets.map(s => [s.name, s.count])), [["Paper 1A — Structure", 5], ["Paper 1B — Data", 2]]);
+  assert.equal(n1.sets[0].paperStyle, "1A");
+  assert.equal(n1.sets[0].calculatorFree, true);
+  assert.equal(n1.sets[1].paperStyle, "1B");
+  assert.equal(n1.sets[1].calculatorFree, false);
+  assert.equal(nodes[1].count, 3);
+  assert.equal(nodes[1].sets.length, 1);
+  assert.equal(QE.buildTopics([a])[0].sets[0].ref, a, "sets keep a reference to the underlying bank object");
+});
+
+test("buildTopics: inline uploaded banks aggregate items like bankItems maps them", () => {
+  const { QE } = engine();
+  const b = {
+    inline: true, label: "My enzymes bank", topicLabel: "Enzymes", topic: "enzymes",
+    items: [
+      { id: "e1", format: "mcq", stem: "s1", choices: ["a", "b"], answer: 0 },
+      { id: "e2", format: "written", stem: "s2", marks: 2, markscheme: ["p (1)"], topic: "kinetics", topicLabel: "Kinetics", difficulty: "hard" },
+      { id: "e3", format: "mcq", stem: "s3", choices: ["a", "b"], answer: 1, retired: true },
+      { id: "e4", format: "mcq", stem: "s4", choices: ["a", "b"], answer: 0, difficulty: "bogus" },
+      { id: "e5", format: "mcq", stem: "s5", choices: ["a", "b"], answer: 0, difficulty: "standard" }
+    ],
+    codes: ["C1.1"]
+  };
+  const nodes = QE.buildTopics([b]);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(nodes.map(n => n.label)), ["Enzymes", "Kinetics"], "sorted by label: effective topicLabels ('Enzymes' < 'Kinetics')");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(nodes.map(n => n.topicKey)), ["enzymes", "kinetics"], "topicKeys are the effective topic keys");
+  const enz = nodes[0];
+  assert.equal(enz.count, 3, "retired item skipped, the rest counted (e1, e4, e5)");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(enz.difficulties), { unrated: 2, easy: 0, standard: 1, hard: 0 }, "invalid difficulty pools as unrated");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(enz.codes), ["C1.1"], "bank-level codes reach the node");
+  assert.equal(enz.label, "Enzymes", "bank topicLabel is the effective item topicLabel");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(enz.sets), [{ ref: b, name: "My enzymes bank", order: null, count: 3, paperStyle: null, calculatorFree: false }]);
+  const kin = nodes[1];
+  assert.equal(kin.count, 1);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(kin.difficulties), { unrated: 0, easy: 0, standard: 0, hard: 1 });
+  assert.equal(kin.label, "Kinetics", "item-level topicLabel wins over the bank's");
+});
+
+test("buildTopics: a bank without meta (offline fallback) is its own topic with unknown count", () => {
+  const { QE } = engine();
+  const fb = { file: "library/questions/bio-unity-diversity-cells.json", label: "Unity & diversity — Cells" };
+  const nodes = QE.buildTopics([fb]);
+  assert.equal(nodes.length, 1);
+  assert.equal(nodes[0].topicKey, "bio-unity-diversity-cells", "topicKey = bankIdFor(bank)");
+  assert.equal(nodes[0].label, "Unity & diversity — Cells");
+  assert.equal(nodes[0].count, null, "count unknown without fetching the file → '?' in the picker");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(nodes[0].difficulties), { unrated: 0, easy: 0, standard: 0, hard: 0 });
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(nodes[0].sets), [{ ref: fb, name: "Unity & diversity — Cells", order: null, count: null, paperStyle: null, calculatorFree: false }]);
+});
+
+test("buildTopics: topics sort by label numerically; sets sort by order (nulls last) then name", () => {
+  const { QE } = engine();
+  const banks = [
+    metaBank("library/questions/b.json", "B", [{ topic: "t-b", topicLabel: "B topic", count: 1 }]),
+    metaBank("library/questions/a10.json", "A10", [{ topic: "t-a10", topicLabel: "A 10", count: 1 }]),
+    metaBank("library/questions/a2.json", "A2", [{ topic: "t-a2", topicLabel: "A 2", count: 1 }])
+  ];
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.buildTopics(banks).map(n => n.label)), ["A 2", "A 10", "B topic"], "numeric-aware label compare");
+
+  const s2 = Object.assign(metaBank("library/questions/s2.json", "Set B", [{ topic: "t1", count: 1 }]), { set: "Practice set 2", setOrder: 2 });
+  const s1 = Object.assign(metaBank("library/questions/s1.json", "Set A", [{ topic: "t1", count: 1 }]), { set: "Practice set 1", setOrder: 1 });
+  const s3 = metaBank("library/questions/s3.json", "Set C", [{ topic: "t1", count: 1 }]);
+  const sets = plain(QE.buildTopics([s2, s1, s3])[0].sets.map(s => s.name));
+  assert.deepEqual(sets, ["Practice set 1", "Practice set 2", "Set C"], "order wins, missing order last");
+});

+test("buildTopics: empty/degenerate input never throws", () => {
+  const { QE } = engine();
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.buildTopics([])), []);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.buildTopics(null)), []);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.buildTopics([null, {}])), []);
+});

+test("buildTopics: bank codes attach only to the declared topic node; meta.set names the set", () => {

+    "the declared-topic node carries the codes; the bank's other topic stays empty (no leak)");

+// ── taughtState ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+test("taughtState: a stored override always wins, in both directions", () => {
+  const { QE } = engine();
+  const today = "2026-10-01"; // C1.1's unit hasn't finished, A1.1's has
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.taughtState("x", ["C1.1"], SCHED, "biology-hl", today, { x: { taught: true, t: 1 } })),
+    { state: "taught", source: "you" }, "override true beats the schedule's not-yet");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.taughtState("x", ["A1.1"], SCHED, "biology-hl", today, { x: { taught: false, t: 1 } })),
+    { state: "not-taught", source: "you" }, "override false beats the schedule's taught");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.taughtState("x", ["A1.1"], SCHED, "biology-hl", today, { x: { taught: "yes", t: 1 } })),
+    { state: "taught", source: "schedule" }, "a non-boolean override is ignored");
+});

+test("taughtState: schedule suggestion — every code covered by a finished unit", () => {
+  const { QE } = engine();
+  const today = "2026-10-01";
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.taughtState("bio-a1-1", ["A1.1"], SCHED, "biology-hl", today, {})), { state: "taught", source: "schedule" });
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.taughtState("bio-c1-1", ["C1.1"], SCHED, "biology-hl", today, {})), { state: "not-taught", source: "schedule" }, "unit ends after today");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.taughtState("x", ["A1.1", "C1.1"], SCHED, "biology-hl", today, {})), { state: "not-taught", source: "schedule" }, "EVERY code must be covered");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.taughtState("x", ["A1.1", "C1.1"], SCHED, "biology-hl", "2026-12-01", {})), { state: "taught", source: "schedule" });
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.taughtState("x", ["Z9.9"], SCHED, "biology-hl", today, {})), { state: "not-taught", source: "schedule" }, "code in no unit");
+});

+test("taughtState: unknown on any doubt (no codes, no schedule, wrong subject, malformed input)", () => {
+  const { QE } = engine();
+  const unknown = { state: "unknown", source: "none" };
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.taughtState("x", [], SCHED, "biology-hl", "2026-10-01", {})), unknown);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.taughtState("x", ["A1.1"], null, "biology-hl", "2026-10-01", {})), unknown);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.taughtState("x", ["A1.1"], SCHED, "math-aa-sl", "2026-10-01", {})), unknown, "subject not tracked by the schedule");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.taughtState("x", "A1.1", SCHED, "biology-hl", "2026-10-01", {})), unknown, "codes must be an array");
+  for (const bad of [undefined, "junk", 42, { subjects: "nope" }, { subjects: { "biology-hl": { units: "nope" } } }, { subjects: { "biology-hl": {} } }]) {
+    assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.taughtState("x", ["A1.1"], bad, "biology-hl", "2026-10-01", {})), unknown, "schedule " + JSON.stringify(bad));
+  }
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.taughtState("x", ["A1.1"], SCHED, "biology-hl", "2026-10-01", "junk")), { state: "taught", source: "schedule" }, "malformed overrides fall through to the schedule");
+});

+test("taughtState: accepts a single subject entry; subject ids compare canonically", () => {

+  // class-schedule.json predates the id scheme ("maths-aa-sl") — canonical compare via profile.js

+  assert.deepEqual(plain(QE.taughtState("m1", ["1.1"], sched, "math-aa-sl", "2026-03-01", {})), { state: "taught", source: "schedule" }, "legacy id matched canonically");

+// ── difficultyPass ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+test("difficultyPass: range boundaries, invalid difficulty ranks as unrated", () => {
+  const { QE } = engine();
+  const rank = (d, min, max) => QE.difficultyPass(d == null ? {} : { difficulty: d }, min, max);
+  assert.equal(rank("unrated", "unrated", "hard"), true, "full range includes unrated");
+  assert.equal(rank("easy", "easy", "standard"), true, "min boundary included");
+  assert.equal(rank("standard", "easy", "standard"), true, "max boundary included");
+  assert.equal(rank("hard", "easy", "standard"), false);
+  assert.equal(rank("unrated", "easy", "standard"), false);
+  assert.equal(rank("easy", "unrated", "unrated"), false, "unrated-only range excludes rated items");
+  assert.equal(rank(null, "easy", "standard"), false, "missing difficulty → unrated");
+  assert.equal(rank("bogus", "easy", "standard"), false, "invalid difficulty → unrated");
+  assert.equal(rank("hard", "unrated", "hard"), true);
+});
+
+test("difficultyPass: crossed-over min/max are swapped, not silently empty", () => {
+  const { QE } = engine();
+  assert.equal(QE.difficultyPass({ difficulty: "easy" }, "standard", "easy"), true);
+  assert.equal(QE.difficultyPass({ difficulty: "standard" }, "standard", "easy"), true);
+  assert.equal(QE.difficultyPass({ difficulty: "hard" }, "standard", "easy"), false);
+  assert.equal(QE.difficultyPass({ difficulty: "unrated" }, "standard", "easy"), false);
+});

+
+// A seeded bank whose items span TWO topics — the Paper 1A/1B shape. No bank-level
+// topic in the manifest (like the real Paper 1A/1B entries), so the codes guard
+// keeps both node codes empty and note-id matching supplies them.
+const TWO_MANIFEST = [
+  { file: "library/questions/chem-two-x.json", subject: "chemistry-hl", subjectLabel: "Chemistry HL", label: "Two-topic bank",
+    codes: ["S1.1"], paperStyle: "1B",
+    counts: { mcq: 3, written: 0, total: 3 },
+    topics: [
+      { topic: "chem-s1-1", topicLabel: "Structure 1.1 — Moles", count: 2, difficulty: { unrated: 1, easy: 1, standard: 0, hard: 0 } },
+      { topic: "chem-s1-9", topicLabel: "Zzz untaught", count: 1, difficulty: { unrated: 1, easy: 0, standard: 0, hard: 0 } }
+    ] }
+];
+const TWO_FILES = {
+  "library/questions/chem-two-x.json": { subject: "chemistry-hl", topic: "chem-two-x", items: [
+    { id: "t1", format: "mcq", stem: "s", choices: ["x", "y"], answer: 0, topic: "chem-s1-1" },
+    { id: "t2", format: "mcq", stem: "s", choices: ["x", "y"], answer: 0, topic: "chem-s1-1", difficulty: "easy" },
+    { id: "t3", format: "mcq", stem: "s", choices: ["x", "y"], answer: 0, topic: "chem-s1-9" } ] }
+};
+const TWO_NOTES = { notes: [
+  { id: "chem-s1-1", subject: "chemistry-hl", code: "S1.1" },
+  { id: "chem-s1-9", subject: "chemistry-hl", code: "S9.9" } ] };
+
+// a Paper 1B bank that ALSO sets calculatorFree — the badge must stay Paper 1B
+const BF_MANIFEST = [
+  { file: "library/questions/chem-bf-x.json", subject: "chemistry-hl", subjectLabel: "Chemistry HL", label: "1B calc-free bank",
+    paperStyle: "1B", calculatorFree: true, counts: { mcq: 1, written: 0, total: 1 },
+    topics: [{ topic: "chem-bf-1", topicLabel: "Badge topic", count: 1, difficulty: { unrated: 1, easy: 0, standard: 0, hard: 0 } }] }
+];
+const BF_FILES = {
+  "library/questions/chem-bf-x.json": { subject: "chemistry-hl", topic: "chem-bf-1", items: [
+    { id: "bf1", format: "mcq", stem: "s", choices: ["x", "y"], answer: 0, topic: "chem-bf-1" } ] }
+};
+
+// the offline fallback: the engine's FALLBACK_BANKS math files, served by the mock
+const FALLBACK_FILES = {
+  "library/questions/math-aa-number-algebra.json": { subject: "math-aa-sl", topic: "math-na", items: [
+    { id: "n1", format: "mcq", stem: "s", choices: ["x", "y"], answer: 0 },
+    { id: "n2", format: "mcq", stem: "s", choices: ["x", "y"], answer: 0 }] },
+  "library/questions/math-aa-functions-2-1-to-2-3.json": { subject: "math-aa-sl", topic: "math-fn", items: [
+    { id: "f1", format: "mcq", stem: "s", choices: ["x", "y"], answer: 0 }] },
+  "library/questions/math-functions-2-4-to-2-5.json": { subject: "math-aa-sl", topic: "math-tf", items: [
+    { id: "g1", format: "mcq", stem: "s", choices: ["x", "y"], answer: 0 }] }
+};
+
+function addEl(env, id) {
+  const el = env.document.createElement("div");
+  el.id = id;
+  env.document.body.appendChild(el);
+  return el;
+}
+
+async function bootWith(opts) {
+  opts = opts || {};
+  const manifest = opts.manifest || QMANIFEST;
+  const notes = opts.notes || NOTES;
+  const schedule = opts.schedule === undefined ? SCHEDULE : opts.schedule;
+  const manifestOk = opts.manifestOk !== false;
+  const files = Object.assign({}, BANK_FILES, opts.files || {});
+  const fetch = (url) => {
+    const p = String(url).split("?")[0].replace(/^.*?(library\/)/, "$1");
+    if (p === "library/questions/manifest.json") return manifestOk ? Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: async () => manifest }) : Promise.reject(new Error("manifest offline"));
+    if (p === "library/topic-notes/manifest.json") return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: async () => notes });
+    if (p === "library/class-schedule.json") return Promise.resolve({ ok: !!schedule, json: async () => schedule || {} });
+    if (files[p]) return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: async () => files[p] });
+    return Promise.reject(new Error("unexpected fetch " + url));
+  };
+  const env = makeEnv({ fetch, readyState: "loading", data: opts.data });
+  // q-sure/q-guess/q-conf: renderQuestion wires these by id after writing innerHTML
+  // (the fake DOM doesn't parse markup, so they must exist as elements up front)
+  ["q-subject", "q-topic", "q-set", "q-format", "q-length", "q-diff-min", "q-diff-max",
+   "q-taught-only", "q-taught-panel", "q-taught-list", "q-start", "q-error",
+   "q-progress", "q-box", "setup", "runner", "summary", "q-log",
+   "q-conf", "q-sure", "q-guess"].forEach(id => addEl(env, id));
+  env.load("store.js").load("quiz-engine.js");
+  env.fire("document", "DOMContentLoaded");
+  await tick(40); // manifest fetch + the background teaching-meta fetch + re-render
+  return env;
+}
+
+const bootPicker = (data) => bootWith({ data });
+
+const options = (el) => plain(el.children.filter(c => c.tagName === "OPTION").map(c => [c.value, c.textContent]));
+const fireChange = (el) => (el.listeners.change || []).forEach(fn => fn());
+const panelSelects = (env) => env.document.getElementById("q-taught-list").children
+  .map(row => row.children.find(c => c.tagName === "SELECT"));
+
+test("picker wiring: topic options carry topicKey values, counts and teaching suffixes", async () => {
+  const env = await bootPicker();
+  const subj = env.document.getElementById("q-subject");
+  subj.value = "chemistry-hl";
+  fireChange(subj);
+  const opts = options(env.document.getElementById("q-topic"));
+  assert.deepEqual(opts, [
+    ["*", "Mixed — all topics (revision)"],
+    ["chem-s1-1", "Structure 1.1 — Moles · 4 Qs · taught"],
+    ["chem-s9-9", "Zzz untaught topic · 1 Qs"]
+  ], "values are topicKeys (not bank indexes); 'taught' comes from the finished schedule unit; unknown shows no suffix");
+  assert.equal(env.document.getElementById("q-set").style.display, "none", "set select hidden while Mixed is selected");
+  assert.equal(env.document.getElementById("q-set").disabled, true);
+});
+
+test("picker wiring: choosing a topic fills #q-set with per-set counts and paper badges", async () => {
+  const env = await bootPicker();
+  const subj = env.document.getElementById("q-subject");
+  subj.value = "chemistry-hl";
+  fireChange(subj);
+  const topic = env.document.getElementById("q-topic");
+  topic.value = "chem-s1-1";
+  fireChange(topic); // the topic change listener re-syncs the set select
+  const set = env.document.getElementById("q-set");
+  assert.equal(set.disabled, false);
+  assert.equal(set.style.display, "");
+  assert.deepEqual(options(set), [
+    ["*", "All sets in this topic"],
+    ["0", "Paper 1A — Structure · 3 Qs — Paper 1A · calculator-free"],
+    ["1", "Paper 1B — Data · 1 Qs — Paper 1B · data practice (calculator allowed)"]
+  ]);
+  topic.value = "*";
+  fireChange(topic);
+  assert.equal(set.style.display, "none", "back to Mixed hides it again");
+});
+
+test("picker wiring: the taught panel lists topics with Auto/Taught/Not yet", async () => {
+  const env = await bootPicker();
+  const subj = env.document.getElementById("q-subject");
+  subj.value = "chemistry-hl";
+  fireChange(subj);
+  const selects = panelSelects(env);
+  assert.equal(selects.length, 2, "one row per topic");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(selects.map(s => s.value)), ["auto", "auto"], "no stored overrides → Auto");
+  const hints = plain(env.document.getElementById("q-taught-list").children.map(r => r.children.find(c => c.className === "q-taught-hint").textContent));
+  assert.deepEqual(hints, ["suggested from class schedule", ""], "schedule-sourced rows show the suggestion hint, the rest none");
+});

+test("picker wiring: setting an override stores {taught, t}; Auto deletes it", async () => {
+  const env = await bootPicker();
+  const subj = env.document.getElementById("q-subject");
+  subj.value = "chemistry-hl";
+  fireChange(subj);
+  let selects = panelSelects(env);
+  selects[0].value = "not-taught";
+  fireChange(selects[0]);
+  const stored = plain(env.NB.store.get("topic-taught", {}));
+  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(stored), ["chem-s1-1"]);
+  assert.equal(stored["chem-s1-1"].taught, false);
+  assert.ok(typeof stored["chem-s1-1"].t === "number");
+  selects = panelSelects(env); // re-rendered
+  assert.equal(selects[0].value, "not-taught", "the panel shows the stored override");
+  selects[0].value = "auto";
+  fireChange(selects[0]);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(env.NB.store.get("topic-taught", {})), {}, "Auto deletes the override");
+});

+test("picker wiring: startSession honours the set select", async () => {

+test("picker wiring: taught-only keeps taught + unknown topics; overrides exclude", async () => {
+  const env = await bootPicker();
+  const subj = env.document.getElementById("q-subject");
+  subj.value = "chemistry-hl";
+  fireChange(subj);

+  env.document.getElementById("q-length").value = "20";

+  let selects = panelSelects(env);
+  selects[0].value = "not-taught";
+  fireChange(selects[0]);
+  await start();
+  await tick(10);
+  assert.match(env.document.getElementById("q-progress").textContent, /Question 1 \/ 1/, "only the untaught/unknown topic's item");
+});

+test("picker wiring: difficulty range filters items; an empty result shows the extended message", async () => {

+  env.document.getElementById("q-diff-min").value = "hard";
+  env.document.getElementById("q-diff-max").value = "hard";

+  assert.match(env.document.getElementById("q-error").textContent, /No questions match those filters — try widening the difficulty range or including not-yet-taught topics\./);
+});

+// ── topic scoping of sessions (P1 regression: multi-topic banks must not leak) ──
+test("picker wiring: a topic pick serves exactly that topic's items of a multi-topic bank", async () => {
+  const env = await bootWith({ manifest: TWO_MANIFEST, notes: TWO_NOTES, files: TWO_FILES });
+  const subj = env.document.getElementById("q-subject");
+  subj.value = "chemistry-hl";

+  env.document.getElementById("q-length").value = "20";
+  const start = env.document.getElementById("q-start").listeners.click[0];
+  topic.value = "chem-s1-9";
+  fireChange(topic);
+  await start();
+  await tick(10);
+  assert.match(env.document.getElementById("q-progress").textContent, /Question 1 \/ 1/,
+    "only the chem-s1-9 item — the bank's chem-s1-1 items stay out despite sharing one bank");
+  topic.value = "chem-s1-1";
+  fireChange(topic);
+  await start();
+  await tick(10);
+  assert.match(env.document.getElementById("q-progress").textContent, /Question 1 \/ 2/, "exactly the two chem-s1-1 items");
+});

+test("picker wiring: taught-only Mixed excludes a not-taught topic's items of a shared bank", async () => {
+  const env = await bootWith({ manifest: TWO_MANIFEST, notes: TWO_NOTES, files: TWO_FILES });
+  const subj = env.document.getElementById("q-subject");
+  subj.value = "chemistry-hl";

+    ["chem-s1-9", "Zzz untaught · 1 Qs · not yet taught"]
+  ], "chem-s1-1 taught via its note code + finished unit; chem-s1-9's unit ends in 2099");
+  env.document.getElementById("q-taught-only").checked = true;
+  env.document.getElementById("q-format").value = "mixed";

+});

+test("picker wiring: a fallback bank (offline, no meta) still serves whole under taught-only Mixed", async () => {
+  const env = await bootWith({ manifestOk: false, files: FALLBACK_FILES });
+  const subj = env.document.getElementById("q-subject");
+  subj.value = "math-aa-sl";

+  assert.ok(opts.some(o => o[0] === "math-aa-number-algebra" && /· \? Qs$/.test(o[1])),
+    "fallback topics show unknown counts: " + JSON.stringify(opts));

+  env.document.getElementById("q-format").value = "mixed";
+  env.document.getElementById("q-length").value = "20";
+  await (env.document.getElementById("q-start").listeners.click[0])();
+  await tick(10);

+    "fallback banks are taken whole — topic-key matching must not empty an offline session");
+});

+test("picker wiring: Paper 1B wins over calculatorFree in the set badge", async () => {

+});

+// node --test tests/js — page-level "My resources" (assets/resources.js over the
+// shared synced "my-res" key): validation, tombstone deletes, filters, deep links.
+// The key's sync/merge/backup rules are covered in myres.test.js; this file pins
+// the engine contract and that resources never touch quiz/card storage.
+"use strict";
+const test = require("node:test");
+const assert = require("node:assert/strict");
+const { makeEnv, FakeStorage, plain } = require("./harness");
+
+const J = JSON.stringify;
+const mk = (data) => makeEnv({ storage: new FakeStorage(data) }).load("store.js").load("profile.js").load("resources.js");
+const R = (e) => e.NB.resources;
+
+test("add: validates, tags and stores into my-res; quiz/card storage untouched", () => {
+  const e = mk();
+  const res = R(e).add({ label: "Khan academy water", url: "https://example.org/water", subject: "biology-hl", topic: "a1-1", type: "video", comment: "osmosis at 4:30" });
+  assert.ok(res.ok, res.error);
+  const map = plain(e.NB.store.get("my-res"));
+  const rec = map[res.id];
+  assert.equal(rec.label, "Khan academy water");
+  assert.equal(rec.subject, "biology-hl");
+  assert.equal(rec.topic, "a1-1");
+  assert.equal(rec.type, "video");
+  assert.equal(rec.comment, "osmosis at 4:30");
+  assert.equal(typeof rec.t, "number");
+  assert.equal(rec.note, undefined, "no note link unless asked for");
+  assert.ok(e.NB.store.isSynced("my-res"));
+  assert.equal(e.NB.store.get("q-userbanks", null), null, "resources never become quiz banks");
+  assert.equal(e.NB.store.get("fc-userdecks", null), null, "resources never become card decks");
+});
+
+test("add/update validation: urls scheme-locked, subject required, legacy ids canonical, lengths capped", () => {
+  const e = mk();
+  const r = R(e);
+  assert.ok(r.add({ label: "x", url: "javascript:alert(1)", subject: "biology-hl" }).error, "javascript: refused");
+  assert.ok(r.add({ label: "x", url: "data:text/html;base64,AAAA", subject: "biology-hl" }).error, "data: refused");
+  assert.ok(r.add({ label: "x", url: "library/notes/a.pdf", subject: "biology-hl" }).error, "relative paths refused");
+  assert.ok(r.add({ label: "x", url: "https://a.b/c" }).error, "subject required");
+  assert.ok(r.add({ label: "", url: "https://a.b/c", subject: "biology-hl" }).error, "title required");
+  const long = "y".repeat(250);
+  const ok = r.add({ label: long, url: "https://a.b/" + "z".repeat(1980), subject: "maths-aa-sl", comment: long, topic: "t".repeat(130) });
+  assert.ok(ok.ok, ok.error);
+  const rec = plain(e.NB.store.get("my-res"))[ok.id];
+  assert.equal(rec.label.length, 200, "title capped");
+  assert.equal(rec.subject, "math-aa-sl", "legacy subject id canonicalised");
+  assert.equal(rec.comment.length, 250, "note kept (under the 500 cap)");
+  assert.equal(rec.topic.length, 120, "topic capped");
+  assert.equal(rec.url.length, "https://a.b/".length + 1980, "url kept verbatim when within limits");
+  const tooLongUrl = r.add({ label: "too long", url: "https://a.b/" + "z".repeat(2100), subject: "math-aa-sl" });
+  assert.ok(tooLongUrl.error, "url > 2000 refused");
+});
+
+test("update: bumps t past the old record, keeps the note link, refuses missing ids and bad input", () => {
+  const e = mk();
+  const r = R(e);
+  const a = r.add({ label: "A", url: "https://x/a", subject: "biology-hl", topic: "a1-1", note: "bio-a1-1" });
+  const before = plain(e.NB.store.get("my-res"))[a.id].t;
+  const u1 = r.update(a.id, { label: "A2", url: "https://x/a2", subject: "biology-hl", topic: "a1-1" });
+  assert.ok(u1.ok, u1.error);
+  const rec = plain(e.NB.store.get("my-res"))[a.id];
+  assert.equal(rec.label, "A2");
+  assert.ok(rec.t > before, "edit must win the newest-t merge");
+  assert.equal(rec.note, "bio-a1-1", "edits keep the note link");
+  assert.ok(r.update("nope", { label: "B", url: "https://x/b", subject: "biology-hl" }).error, "unknown id refused");
+  assert.ok(r.update(a.id, { label: "C", url: "ftp://x/c", subject: "biology-hl" }).error, "bad url refused");
+});
+
+test("update: the page's row editor sends no subject — it keeps the record's own; note-scoped records stay note-scoped", () => {
+  const e = mk();
+  const r = R(e);
+  // exactly the payload resources.js wireRow sends (no subject field)
+  const a = r.add({ label: "A", url: "https://x/a", subject: "chemistry-hl", topic: "s1-1" });
+  const u = r.update(a.id, { label: "A2", url: "https://x/a2", type: "video", topic: "s1-2", comment: "", note: undefined });
+  assert.ok(u.ok, "row-editor edit without a subject must succeed: " + u.error);
+  const rec = plain(e.NB.store.get("my-res"))[a.id];
+  assert.equal(rec.subject, "chemistry-hl", "subject preserved from the record");
+  assert.equal(rec.type, "video");
+  assert.equal(rec.note, undefined, "undefined note on a record without one stays absent");
+  // a note-scoped record (no subject, as written by the notes page) must stay editable
+  const e2 = mk({ "my-res": J({ n1: { note: "bio-a1-1", label: "Class notes", url: "https://x/n", t: 10 } }) });
+  const u2 = R(e2).update("n1", { label: "Class notes 2", url: "https://x/n2", type: "doc", topic: "", comment: "", note: undefined });
+  assert.ok(u2.ok, "note-scoped record edit must succeed: " + u2.error);
+  const rec2 = plain(e2.NB.store.get("my-res")).n1;
+  assert.equal(rec2.note, "bio-a1-1", "note link kept");
+  assert.equal(rec2.subject, undefined, "no subject invented");
+  // an explicit empty subject on a tagged record is still a user error
+  const e3 = mk();
+  const b = R(e3).add({ label: "B", url: "https://x/b", subject: "biology-hl" });
+  assert.ok(R(e3).update(b.id, { label: "B2", url: "https://x/b2", subject: "" }).error, "explicit empty subject refused");
+});
+
+test("remove: tombstone wins the sync merge and keeps context; resource list excludes it", () => {
+  const e = mk();
+  const r = R(e);
+  const a = r.add({ label: "A", url: "https://x/a", subject: "chemistry-hl", topic: "s1-1", note: "chem-s1-1" });
+  const t0 = plain(e.NB.store.get("my-res"))[a.id].t;
+  assert.equal(r.remove(a.id), true);
+  const tomb = plain(e.NB.store.get("my-res"))[a.id];
+  assert.equal(tomb.deleted, true);
+  assert.ok(tomb.t > t0, "tombstone must be newer than the record it kills");
+  assert.equal(tomb.subject, "chemistry-hl", "context kept for the tombstone");
+  assert.equal(tomb.note, "chem-s1-1");
+  assert.equal(tomb.label, undefined, "no payload left in a tombstone");
+  // per-item merge (store.js): an older server copy without the delete must not resurrect it
+  const server = { [a.id]: { label: "A", url: "https://x/a", subject: "chemistry-hl", topic: "s1-1", t: t0 } };
+  const merged = plain(e.NB.merge.RECORDS["my-res"](plain(e.NB.store.get("my-res")), server));
+  assert.equal(merged[a.id].deleted, true, "older server copy cannot resurrect a deleted resource");
+  const f = r.filterRecords(merged, {});
+  assert.equal(f.length, 0, "deleted records are not listed");
+  assert.equal(r.remove("nope"), false);
+});
+
+test("filterRecords: subject/type/search filters, profile hiding, newest first", () => {
+  const e = mk({
+    "profile": J({ subjects: ["biology-hl"] }), // account profiled to Biology only
+    "my-res": J({
+      b2: { label: "Bio video", url: "https://x/b", subject: "biology-hl", type: "video", t: 10 },
+      b1: { label: "Bio notes", url: "https://x/n", subject: "biology-hl", type: "doc", comment: "class scan", t: 20 },
+      c1: { label: "Chem site", url: "https://x/c", subject: "chemistry-hl", t: 30 },
+      gone: { deleted: true, subject: "biology-hl", t: 40 },
+      broken: { label: "No url", url: "javascript:x", subject: "biology-hl", t: 50 },
+    }),
+  });
+  const r = R(e);
+  const map = plain(e.NB.store.get("my-res"));
+  // the page renders sortRecords(filterRecords(...)) — unfiltered order is insertion order
+  const all = r.sortRecords(r.filterRecords(map, {}));
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(all.map(([id]) => id)), ["b1", "b2"], "profile hides chemistry; tombstones/invalid excluded; newest first");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(r.filterRecords(map, { subject: "chemistry-hl" }).map(([id]) => id)), [], "explicit subject filter still respects the profile");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(r.filterRecords(map, { type: "doc" }).map(([id]) => id)), ["b1"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(r.filterRecords(map, { q: "class scan" }).map(([id]) => id)), ["b1"], "search covers the note text");
+});
+
+test("topic index and deep links: manifest drives the datalist, note link and the AI picker", () => {
+  const e = mk();
+  const r = R(e);
+  r.setTopicIndex({ notes: [
+    { file: "library/topic-notes/biology-hl/a1-2.json", id: "bio-a1-2", subject: "biology-hl", code: "A1.2", title: "Nucleic acids" },
+    { file: "library/topic-notes/biology-hl/a1-1.json", id: "bio-a1-1", subject: "biology-hl", code: "A1.1", title: "Water" },
+  ] });
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(r.topicsFor("biology-hl").map((t) => t.key)), ["a1-1", "a1-2"], "sorted by code");
+  assert.equal(r.noteIdFor("biology-hl", "a1-1"), "bio-a1-1");
+  assert.equal(r.noteIdFor("biology-hl", "zz"), "");
+  const rec = { subject: "biology-hl", topic: "a1-1" };
+  assert.equal(r.notesUrl(rec), "notes.html?subject=biology-hl&topic=a1-1");
+  assert.equal(r.makeQuestionsUrl(rec), "author.html?ai-subject=biology-hl&ai-topic=a1-1");
+  assert.equal(r.makeQuestionsUrl({ subject: "chemistry-hl" }), "author.html?ai-subject=chemistry-hl");
+  assert.equal(r.notesUrl({ subject: "chemistry-hl" }), "", "no topic → no notes deep link");
+  assert.equal(r.makeQuestionsUrl({ topic: "a1-1" }), "", "no subject → no AI link");
+});

+// node --test tests/js — the "topic-taught" store key: sync registration, per-user
+// scoping, dirty marking, the RECORDS merge (per-topic newest t wins) and the
+// backup.js shape validator.
+"use strict";
+const test = require("node:test");
+const assert = require("node:assert/strict");
+const { makeEnv, plain } = require("./harness");
+
+const J = JSON.stringify;
+
+function env(data) { return makeEnv({ data }).load("store.js"); }
+function backupEnv(data) { return makeEnv({ data }).load("store.js").load("backup.js"); }
+
+test("topic-taught is a synced key and round-trips through NB.store", () => {
+  const e = env({});
+  const s = e.NB.store;
+  assert.ok(s.SYNCED_KEYS.indexOf("topic-taught") >= 0, "listed in SYNCED_KEYS");
+  assert.ok(s.isSynced("topic-taught"), "isSynced");
+  assert.equal(s.set("topic-taught", { "bio-a1-1": { taught: true, t: 5 } }), true);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(s.get("topic-taught", {})), { "bio-a1-1": { taught: true, t: 5 } });
+  assert.ok(s.dirty()["topic-taught"] > 0, "a non-silent write marks the key for sync");
+});
+
+test("topic-taught stays per-user scoped", () => {
+  const e = env({});
+  const s = e.NB.store;
+  assert.equal(s.setScope("u1"), true);
+  assert.equal(s.set("topic-taught", { t1: { taught: true, t: 1 } }), true);
+  assert.equal(e.storage.getItem("u1:topic-taught"), J({ t1: { taught: true, t: 1 } }), "physically scoped");
+  assert.equal(e.storage.getItem("topic-taught"), null, "nothing under the plain key");
+  assert.equal(s.setScope("u2"), true);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(s.get("topic-taught", {})), {}, "u2 does not see u1's overrides");
+  assert.equal(s.setScope("u1"), true);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(s.get("topic-taught", {})), { t1: { taught: true, t: 1 } }, "switching back restores them");
+});
+
+test("NB.merge RECORDS: per-topic newest t wins; every topic survives", () => {
+  const merge = (a, b) => plain(env({}).NB.merge.RECORDS["topic-taught"](a, b));
+  // local {A: taught true, t 5} vs server {A: taught false, t 9; B: taught true, t 1}
+  assert.deepEqual(merge({ A: { taught: true, t: 5 } }, { A: { taught: false, t: 9 }, B: { taught: true, t: 1 } }),
+    { A: { taught: false, t: 9 }, B: { taught: true, t: 1 } }, "A from the server (newer t); B kept");
+  assert.deepEqual(merge({ A: { taught: false, t: 9 } }, { A: { taught: true, t: 5 } }),
+    { A: { taught: false, t: 9 } }, "local wins when its t is newer");
+  assert.deepEqual(merge({ A: { taught: true, t: 5 } }, { A: { taught: false, t: 5 } }),
+    { A: { taught: false, t: 5 } }, "tie → b wins (the RECORDS convention)");
+});
+
+test("NB.merge RECORDS: missing t ranks as t=0; non-object input never crashes", () => {
+  const merge = (a, b) => plain(env({}).NB.merge.RECORDS["topic-taught"](a, b));
+  assert.deepEqual(merge({ A: { taught: true } }, { A: { taught: false, t: 9 } }),
+    { A: { taught: false, t: 9 } }, "untimed local record loses to a timed one");
+  assert.deepEqual(merge({ A: { taught: true, t: 3 } }, { A: { taught: false } }),
+    { A: { taught: true, t: 3 } }, "timed local record beats an untimed one");
+  assert.deepEqual(merge({ A: "junk" }, { A: { taught: true, t: 3 } }), { A: { taught: true, t: 3 } }, "non-object record side loses, no crash");
+  assert.deepEqual(merge({ A: { taught: true, t: 3 } }, { A: "junk" }), { A: { taught: true, t: 3 } });
+  assert.deepEqual(merge(5, { A: { taught: true, t: 1 } }), { A: { taught: true, t: 1 } }, "non-object whole value → other side");
+  assert.deepEqual(merge({ A: { taught: true, t: 1 } }, "x"), { A: { taught: true, t: 1 } });
+  assert.deepEqual(merge(undefined, { A: { taught: true, t: 1 } }), { A: { taught: true, t: 1 } });
+});
+
+test("backup: topic-taught is collected, validated and restored record-by-record", () => {
+  const a = backupEnv({});
+  a.NB.store.set("topic-taught", { t1: { taught: true, t: 5 }, bad: "junk", bad2: { taught: "yes" } });
+  const file = J(a.NB.backup.collect());
+  assert.ok(Object.keys(JSON.parse(file).keys).indexOf("topic-taught") >= 0, "collect includes the key");
+
+  const b = backupEnv({});
+  const p = b.NB.backup.parse(file);
+  assert.equal(p.ok, true, p.errors.join("; "));
+  assert.ok(p.warnings.some(w => /topic-taught/.test(w)), "dropped records are counted as warnings");
+  const r = b.NB.backup.restore(p, "merge");
+  assert.equal(r.ok, true);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(b.NB.store.get("topic-taught", {})), { t1: { taught: true, t: 5 } }, "bad records dropped, good ones kept");
+
+  const c = backupEnv({});
+  const bad = c.NB.backup.parse(J({ format: "nevaib-backup", version: 3, keys: { "topic-taught": [1, 2] } }));
+  assert.equal(bad.ok, false, "a non-object whole value is rejected as a damaged file");
+  assert.match(bad.errors.join(" "), /wrong shape/);
+});

+test("isISO/addDays: padded calendar dates only, day arithmetic crosses months", () => {
+  const t = T();
+  assert.equal(t.isISO("2026-11-20"), true);
+  assert.equal(t.isISO("2026-2-3"), false, "unpadded");
+  assert.equal(t.isISO("2026-02-30"), false, "impossible day");
+  assert.equal(t.isISO("2026-13-01"), false, "impossible month");
+  assert.equal(t.isISO("garbage"), false);
+  assert.equal(t.isISO(42), false); assert.equal(t.isISO(null), false); assert.equal(t.isISO(undefined), false);
+  assert.equal(t.addDays("2026-11-20", 21), "2026-12-11");
+  assert.equal(t.addDays("2026-11-20", 60), "2027-01-19");
+  assert.equal(t.addDays("2026-12-31", 1), "2027-01-01");
+});

+test("activeUnits: from/to boundary dates inclusive; malformed units skipped", () => {
+  const t = T();
+  const sched = { subjects: { "biology-hl": { units: [
+    { from: "2026-11-01", to: "2026-11-20", codes: ["C1.1"], unit: "Enzymes" },
+    { from: "2026-11-21", to: "2026-11-30", codes: ["A2.2"], unit: "Next" },
+    { from: "2026-12-01", codes: ["X"], unit: "no-to" },
+    { to: "2026-12-05", unit: "no-from" },
+    { from: "2027-02-30", to: "2027-03-05", codes: ["X"], unit: "bad-date" },
+    { from: "2026-01-01", to: "2027-12-31", codes: "junk", unit: "Wide" }
+  ] } } };
+  const at = (date) => plain(t.activeUnits(sched, [["biology-hl", "Biology HL"]], date)).map(u => u.unit.unit);
+  assert.deepEqual(at("2026-11-01"), ["Wide", "Enzymes"], "from boundary included");
+  assert.deepEqual(at("2026-11-20"), ["Wide", "Enzymes"], "to boundary included");
+  assert.deepEqual(at("2026-11-21"), ["Wide", "Next"], "day after the close is the next unit");
+  assert.deepEqual(at("2026-12-01"), ["Wide"], "malformed units (missing dates, impossible date) skipped");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(t.activeUnits(null, [["biology-hl", "B"]], "2026-12-01")), []);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(t.activeUnits(sched, [], "2026-12-01")), []);
+});

+test("nearTermItems / startThinkingItems: windows and boundaries; far-future items appear only in the long lead window", () => {
+  const t = T();
+  const today = "2026-11-20";
+  const mk = (date, label) => ({ date, label, href: "x.html", where: "calendar" });
+  const items = [
+    mk("2026-11-19", "past"),
+    mk(today, "today"),
+    mk("2026-12-11", "lead-edge"),   // today + 21
+    mk("2026-12-12", "just-out"),    // today + 22 → start thinking
+    mk("2027-01-19", "long-edge"),   // today + 60
+    mk("2027-01-20", "too-far"),     // today + 61 → hidden
+    mk("2027-02-17", "ee-launch"),   // far future → hidden today
+    mk("2026-13-01", "bad-month"), mk("nope", "bad"), mk(null, "null")
+  ];
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(t.nearTermItems(items, today, 21)).map(x => x.label), ["today", "lead-edge"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(t.startThinkingItems(items, today, 21, 60)).map(x => x.label), ["just-out", "long-edge"]);
+  // a Feb 2027 item shows up in the long lead window once within 60 days of it
+  const later = "2027-01-10";
+  const feb = [mk("2027-02-17", "ee-launch"), mk("2026-13-01", "bad")];
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(t.nearTermItems(feb, later, 21)).map(x => x.label), [], "still not near-term in January");
+  assert.deepEqual(plain(t.startThinkingItems(feb, later, 21, 60)).map(x => x.label), ["ee-launch"], "EE launch enters the long lead window");
+});

+// panel-level: coming-up block, empty-block hiding, bad data, suggestion rendering
+
+// boot the real render path with NB.deadlines preset (deadlines.js export stand-in)
+const bootDl = async (deadlines, opts) => {
+  const env = makeEnv(Object.assign({ readyState: "complete", fetch: repoFetch() }, opts || {}));
+  env.load("store.js").load("profile.js");
+  const box = env.document.createElement("div");
+  box.id = "today-body";
+  env.document.body.appendChild(box);
+  env.NB.deadlines = deadlines; // read at render time, before the async load settles
+  env.load("today.js");
+  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 80));
+  return { box, env };
+};
+// coursework.json replaced by an empty planner so the dates tested are exactly the injected deadlines
+const noCoursework = (url) => (String(url).includes("coursework")
+  ? Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: async () => ({ tracks: [] }) })
+  : repoFetch()(url));
+const isoOffset = (n) => { const d = new Date(); d.setDate(d.getDate() + n);
+  return d.getFullYear() + "-" + String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0"); };
+
+test("today panel: dated items render near-term, start-thinking label for the long lead window; empty data hides the block entirely", async () => {
+  const { box } = await bootDl([
+    { d: isoOffset(0), t: "Good thing now" },
+    { d: isoOffset(30), t: "Think piece" },
+    { d: isoOffset(90), t: "Too far out" }
+  ], { fetch: noCoursework });
+  assert.match(allText(box), /4\. Coming up/);
+  assert.match(allText(box), /Good thing now/, "near-term item shown");
+  assert.match(allText(box), /\(today\)/, "dated today reads as today");
+  assert.match(allText(box), /Start thinking about Think piece/, "long-lead item labelled 'Start thinking about'");
+  assert.doesNotMatch(allText(box), /Too far out/, "beyond the long lead window → not shown");
+
+  const empty = await bootDl([], { fetch: noCoursework });
+  assert.doesNotMatch(allText(empty.box), /4\. Coming up/, "empty block renders nothing at all");
+  assert.doesNotMatch(allText(empty.box), /Start thinking about/, "no stub for the empty block either");
+});
+
+test("today panel: one malformed record skips itself; schedule, deadlines and the panel still render", async () => {
+  const sched = { subjects: { "biology-hl": { units: [
+    { from: "2000-01-01", to: "2099-12-31", codes: "C1.1", unit: "Wide unit" }, // codes not an array
+    { from: "oops", to: "2099-01-01", codes: [], unit: "Broken unit" },
+    { from: "2027-02-30", to: "2027-03-01", codes: [], unit: "Impossible date" }
+  ] } }, breaks: [{ from: "x", to: "y", label: "Bad break" }] };
+  const fetch = (url) => (String(url).includes("class-schedule")
+    ? Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: async () => sched }) : noCoursework(url));
+  const { box } = await bootDl([{ d: "garbage", t: "Bad row" }, { d: isoOffset(3), t: "Near thing" }], { fetch });
+  assert.doesNotMatch(allText(box), /could not be loaded/, "no malformed record blanks the panel");
+  assert.match(allText(box), /3\. This week in class/, "schedule section renders");
+  assert.match(allText(box), /Wide unit/, "the valid unit shows (codes guard exercised)");
+  assert.doesNotMatch(allText(box), /Broken unit/, "missing from skipped");
+  assert.doesNotMatch(allText(box), /Impossible date/, "impossible date skipped");
+  assert.doesNotMatch(allText(box), /Bad break/, "malformed break skipped");
+  assert.match(allText(box), /4\. Coming up/, "dates block survives the bad deadline row");
+  assert.match(allText(box), /Near thing/, "the valid deadline still shows");
+  assert.doesNotMatch(allText(box), /Bad row/, "the malformed deadline row is gone");
+});
+
+test("today panel: suggestions render ranked with reason and direct link (cards first, then mistakes)", async () => {
+  const data = {
+    "fc-userdecks": J([{ name: "d", subject: "biology-hl", cards: [{ id: "my-1", front: "F", back: "B" }] }]),
+    "fc-state": J({ "my-1": { box: 1, due: "2026-01-01" } }),
+    "q-errors": J([{ key: "bank-x#7", bankId: "bank-x", subject: "biology-hl", topic: "t1", topicLabel: "Enzymes", lastMissed: "2026-11-01" }])
+  };
+  const { box } = await bootDl([{ d: isoOffset(2), t: "Prep thing" }], { data, fetch: noCoursework });
+  const text = allText(box);
+  assert.match(text, /5\. Suggested next step/);
+  const blocks = [];
+  (function walk(el) { (el.children || []).forEach(c => { if (c.className === "td-block") blocks.push(c); walk(c); }); })(box);
+  const sugg = blocks.find(b => b.children[0] && allText(b.children[0]).startsWith("5."));
+  assert.ok(sugg, "suggestions block found");
+  const rows = sugg.children.filter(x => x.className === "td-subj");
+  assert.deepEqual(rows.map(r => allText(r.children[0])).map(s => s.trim()), ["1 card due", "Open mistakes on Enzymes"],
+    "ranked: cards due first, then open mistakes; deadline prep pushed out by the two-item cap");
+  const hrefs = [];
+  (function walk(el) { if (el.tagName === "A" && el.attrs && el.attrs.href) hrefs.push(el.attrs.href); (el.children || []).forEach(walk); })(sugg);
+  assert.ok(hrefs.some(h => h.startsWith("cards.html")), "cards suggestion carries a direct link");
+  assert.ok(hrefs.some(h => h.startsWith("quiz.html?errkeys=")), "mistakes suggestion carries the retry link");
+});

+test("today panel: question manifest and bank files are fetched once, not on every render", async () => {
+  const REPO_Q = path.join(REPO, "library", "questions");
+  const man = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(REPO_Q, "manifest.json"), "utf8"));
+  const bankFile = man[0].file;
+  const bankId = bankFile.split("/").pop().replace(/\.json$/, "");
+  const first = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(REPO, bankFile), "utf8")).items[0];
+  const urls = [];
+  const fetch = (u) => { urls.push(String(u)); return repoFetch()(u); };
+  const env = makeEnv({ readyState: "complete", fetch, data: {
+    "q-errors": J([{ key: bankId + "#" + first.id, id: first.id, bankId: bankId, topic: first.topic, topicLabel: "Cache check", lastMissed: "2026-11-01" }])
+  } });
+  env.load("store.js").load("profile.js");
+  const box = env.document.createElement("div");
+  box.id = "today-body";
+  env.document.body.appendChild(box);
+  env.load("today.js");
+  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 80));
+  env.NB.store.set("quizlog", []); // watched key → debounced re-render
+  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 500));
+  assert.match(allText(box), /Start a 6-question quiz/, "panel re-rendered with the weak topic");
+  assert.equal(urls.filter(u => u.includes("library/questions/manifest.json")).length, 1,
+    "questions manifest fetched once across renders (was: every render)");
+  assert.equal(urls.filter(u => u.includes(bankFile.split("/").pop())).length, 1,
+    "bank file fetched once across renders (was: every render)");
+});

+test("suggestActions: a closing unit without a note does not suppress one that has a link", () => {
+  const t = T();
+  const today = "2026-11-20";
+  const notes = { "biology-hl|C1.1": { subject: "biology-hl", file: "library/topic-notes/biology-hl/c1-1.json", code: "C1.1", title: "Enzymes and metabolism",
+    links: { quizBanks: [{ file: "library/questions/bio-c1-1.json" }] } } };
+  const units = [
+    { subject: "math-aa-sl", label: "Math AA SL", unit: { from: "2026-11-16", to: today, codes: ["5.1"], unit: "Differentiation" } }, // closes today, no note
+    { subject: "biology-hl", label: "Biology HL", unit: { from: "2026-11-02", to: "2026-11-27", codes: ["C1.1"], unit: "Enzymes and metabolism" } }
+  ];
+  const s = plain(t.suggestActions({ today, due: 0, own: 0, errors: [], units, notes, nearItems: [] }));
+  assert.equal(s.length, 1);
+  assert.match(s[0].title, /Unit closes this week/);
+  assert.match(s[0].reason, /Enzymes and metabolism/, "the linkable closing unit is used");
+  assert.match(s[0].href, /^quiz\.html\?from=/);
+});

+test("datedItems: the same event in the calendar and the planner is listed once; distinct same-day events are kept", () => {
+  const t = T();
+  const dl = [
+    { d: "2027-06-15", t: "Biology: IA experiment day (group 1)" },
+    { d: "2027-01-12", t: "Revision-only homework window begins" }
+  ];
+  const cw = { tracks: [{ id: "bio", subject: "biology-hl", label: "Biology", stages: [
+    { id: "data", title: "Data", dates: [
+      { date: "2027-06-15", label: "Biology IA experiment day (group 1)" }, // same event, different punctuation
+      { date: "2027-06-15", label: "Something else entirely" }              // distinct event, same day
+    ] }
+  ] }] };
+  const items = plain(t.datedItems(dl, cw, null));
+  assert.deepEqual(items.map(x => [x.date, x.label]), [
+    ["2027-01-12", "Revision-only homework window begins"],
+    ["2027-06-15", "Biology: IA experiment day (group 1)"],
+    ["2027-06-15", "Something else entirely"]
+  ], "duplicate event dropped (first source wins), distinct same-day events kept");
+});

+test("maths notes resolve across the manifest's legacy 'maths-aa-sl' spelling and a canonical profile", async () => {
+  const sched = { subjects: { "maths-aa-sl": { units: [
+    { from: "2000-01-01", to: isoOffset(3), codes: ["5.1"], unit: "Differentiation" } // closes this week
+  ] } } };
+  const manifest = { notes: [{ subject: "maths-aa-sl", code: "5.1", file: "library/topic-notes/maths-aa-sl/5-1.json" }] };
+  const note = { code: "5.1", title: "Differentiation basics", links: { quizBanks: [{ file: "library/questions/math-aa-1.json", itemIds: [] }] } };
+  const fetch = (url) => {
+    const u = String(url);
+    if (u.includes("class-schedule")) return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: async () => sched });
+    if (u.includes("topic-notes/manifest")) return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: async () => manifest });
+    if (u.includes("topic-notes/maths-aa-sl")) return Promise.resolve({ ok: true, json: async () => note });
+    return repoFetch()(url);
+  };
+  const { box } = await bootRender({ fetch, data: { profile: J({ subjects: ["math-aa-sl"] }) } });
+  const text = allText(box);
+  assert.match(text, /5\.1 Differentiation basics/, "note found via canon-compare despite the legacy manifest spelling");
+  assert.doesNotMatch(text, /No notes written for these codes yet/, "not treated as noteless");
+  assert.match(text, /Unit closes this week/, "the closing maths unit produces a suggestion");
+  assert.match(text, /Differentiation ends/, "suggestion reason names the unit");
+  const hrefs = [];
+  (function walk(el) { if (el.tagName === "A" && el.attrs && el.attrs.href) hrefs.push(el.attrs.href); (el.children || []).forEach(walk); })(box);
+  assert.ok(hrefs.some(h => h.startsWith("quiz.html?from=math-aa-1")), "suggestion links the note's quiz bank");
+});

diff --git a/tests/test_questions_manifest.py b/tests/test_questions_manifest.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..2321e9e

+"""tools/build_questions_manifest.py: keeps manifest labels/order intact, appends
+unlisted banks with a label fallback, computes counts/topics metadata, and --check
+detects drift. Aggregation mirrors bankItems() in assets/quiz-engine.js."""
+
+import json
+import os
+import subprocess
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+TOOL = ROOT / "tools" / "build_questions_manifest.py"
+
+
+def run(root, *args):
+    env = dict(os.environ, NEVAIB_ROOT=str(root))
+    return subprocess.run(
+        [sys.executable, "-I", str(TOOL), *args],
+        capture_output=True,
+        text=True,
+        env=env,
+    )
+
+
+def write_manifest(root, entries):
+    f = root / "library" / "questions" / "manifest.json"
+    f.write_text(
+        json.dumps(entries, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
+    )
+
+
+def read_manifest(root):
+    return json.loads(
+        (root / "library" / "questions" / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    )
+
+
+def write_bank(root, name, subject, items, **extra):
+    (root / "library" / "questions").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    bank = {"subject": subject, "items": items}
+    bank.update(extra)
+    (root / "library" / "questions" / name).write_text(
+        json.dumps(bank, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
+    )
+
+
+def mcq(i, **extra):
+    it = {
+        "id": i,
+        "format": "mcq",
+        "stem": f"Stem {i}?",
+        "choices": ["a", "b"],
+        "answer": 0,
+    }
+    it.update(extra)
+    return it
+
+
+def written(i, **extra):
+    it = {
+        "id": i,
+        "format": "written",
+        "stem": f"Stem {i}?",
+        "marks": 2,
+        "markscheme": ["x"],
+    }
+    it.update(extra)
+    return it
+
+
+def make_root(tmp_path):
+    (tmp_path / "library" / "questions").mkdir(parents=True)
+    return tmp_path
+
+
+def test_preserves_labels_order_and_identity_fields(tmp_path):
+    root = make_root(tmp_path)
+    write_bank(root, "za-a.json", "subj-one", [mcq("q1")], topic="ta", topicLabel="TA")
+    write_bank(
+        root, "za-b.json", "subj-two", [mcq("q1")], topic="tb"
+    )  # subject differs from manifest
+    write_manifest(
+        root,
+        [
+            {
+                "file": "library/questions/za-a.json",
+                "subject": "subj-one",
+                "subjectLabel": "Subject One",
+                "label": "Label A",
+            },
+            {
+                "file": "library/questions/za-b.json",
+                "subject": "subj-one",
+                "subjectLabel": "Subject One",
+                "label": "Label B",
+            },
+        ],
+    )
+    r = run(root)
+    assert r.returncode == 0, r.stdout + r.stderr
+    entries = read_manifest(root)
+    assert [(e["file"], e["label"], e["subjectLabel"]) for e in entries] == [
+        ("library/questions/za-a.json", "Label A", "Subject One"),
+        ("library/questions/za-b.json", "Label B", "Subject One"),
+    ]
+    assert entries[1]["subject"] == "subj-one"  # manifest identity wins over the bank's
+
+
+def test_appends_unlisted_bank_with_label_fallback(tmp_path):
+    root = make_root(tmp_path)
+    write_bank(root, "za-a.json", "subj-one", [mcq("q1")], topic="ta")
+    write_bank(
+        root,
+        "zz-titled.json",
+        "subj-two",
+        [mcq("q1")],
+        topic="tb",
+        topicLabel="Titled bank",
+    )
+    write_bank(root, "zz-bare.json", "subj-two", [mcq("q1")], topic="tc")
+    write_manifest(
+        root,
+        [
+            {
+                "file": "library/questions/za-a.json",
+                "subject": "subj-one",
+                "subjectLabel": "Subject One",
+                "label": "Label A",
+            }
+        ],
+    )
+    r = run(root)
+    assert r.returncode == 0, r.stdout + r.stderr
+    entries = read_manifest(root)
+    assert [e["file"] for e in entries] == [
+        "library/questions/za-a.json",
+        "library/questions/zz-bare.json",
+        "library/questions/zz-titled.json",
+    ]
+    assert entries[2]["label"] == "Titled bank"  # bank topicLabel
+    assert entries[1]["label"] == "zz-bare"  # filename-stem fallback
+    assert entries[2]["subject"] == entries[2]["subjectLabel"] == "subj-two"
+    assert entries[2]["topic"] == "tb" and "topicLabel" not in entries[1]
+
+
+def test_counts_topics_and_difficulty_aggregation(tmp_path):
+    root = make_root(tmp_path)
+    items = [
+        mcq("q1", topic="sub-a", topicLabel="Sub A", difficulty="easy"),
+        written("q2"),  # no item topic -> bank topic; no difficulty -> unrated
+        mcq("q3", topic="sub-b", retired=True, retiredReason="off-syllabus"),
+        mcq("q4", difficulty="brutal"),  # unknown vocabulary -> unrated, never invented
+        mcq("q5", topic="sub-b", difficulty="hard"),
+    ]
+    write_bank(
+        root,
+        "za-a.json",
+        "subj-one",
+        items,
+        topic="bank-topic",
+        topicLabel="Bank topic label",
+        set="Set X",
+        setOrder=3,
+        codes=["C1.1"],
+        paperStyle="1A",
+        calculatorFree=True,
+    )
+    write_manifest(
+        root,
+        [
+            {
+                "file": "library/questions/za-a.json",
+                "subject": "subj-one",
+                "subjectLabel": "Subject One",
+                "label": "Label A",
+            }
+        ],
+    )
+    assert run(root).returncode == 0
+    e = read_manifest(root)[0]
+    assert e["counts"] == {"mcq": 3, "written": 1, "total": 4}  # retired q3 excluded
+    assert e["set"] == "Set X" and e["setOrder"] == 3 and e["codes"] == ["C1.1"]
+    assert e["paperStyle"] == "1A" and e["calculatorFree"] is True
+    assert e["topics"] == [
+        {
+            "topic": "sub-a",
+            "topicLabel": "Sub A",
+            "count": 1,
+            "difficulty": {"unrated": 0, "easy": 1, "standard": 0, "hard": 0},
+        },
+        {
+            "topic": "bank-topic",
+            "topicLabel": "Bank topic label",
+            "count": 2,
+            "difficulty": {"unrated": 2, "easy": 0, "standard": 0, "hard": 0},
+        },
+        {
+            "topic": "sub-b",
+            "topicLabel": "Bank topic label",
+            "count": 1,
+            "difficulty": {"unrated": 0, "easy": 0, "standard": 0, "hard": 1},
+        },
+    ]
+
+
+def test_bank_without_topic_falls_back_to_filename_stem(tmp_path):
+    root = make_root(tmp_path)
+    write_bank(root, "zz-bare.json", "subj-one", [mcq("q1"), written("q2")])
+    r = run(root)  # no manifest at all -> fresh one
+    assert r.returncode == 0, r.stdout + r.stderr
+    e = read_manifest(root)[0]
+    assert e["label"] == "zz-bare"
+    assert e["topics"][0]["topic"] == "zz-bare"
+    assert (
+        e["topics"][0]["topicLabel"] == "zz-bare"
+    )  # label falls back to the topic key
+    assert not any(
+        k in e
+        for k in (
+            "topic",
+            "topicLabel",
+            "set",
+            "setOrder",
+            "codes",
+            "paperStyle",
+            "calculatorFree",
+        )
+    )
+
+
+def test_keeps_entries_for_missing_banks_and_warns(tmp_path):
+    root = make_root(tmp_path)
+    write_manifest(
+        root,
+        [
+            {
+                "file": "library/questions/gone.json",
+                "subject": "s",
+                "subjectLabel": "S",
+                "label": "Gone",
+            }
+        ],
+    )
+    r = run(root, "--check")
+    assert r.returncode == 0, r.stdout + r.stderr
+    assert "WARN" in r.stderr and "gone.json" in r.stderr
+    assert read_manifest(root)[0]["label"] == "Gone"  # manifest stayed stable
+
+
+def test_check_mode_detects_drift_and_passes_when_fresh(tmp_path):
+    root = make_root(tmp_path)
+    write_bank(root, "za-a.json", "subj-one", [mcq("q1")], topic="ta", topicLabel="TA")
+    write_manifest(
+        root,
+        [
+            {
+                "file": "library/questions/za-a.json",
+                "subject": "subj-one",
+                "subjectLabel": "Subject One",
+                "label": "Label A",
+            }
+        ],
+    )
+    r = run(root, "--check")
+    assert r.returncode == 1
+    assert "out of date" in r.stderr and "za-a.json" in r.stderr
+    r = run(root)  # write mode
+    assert r.returncode == 0, r.stdout + r.stderr
+    r = run(root, "--check")  # fresh -> clean
+    assert r.returncode == 0, r.stdout + r.stderr
+    write_bank(
+        root,
+        "za-a.json",
+        "subj-one",
+        [mcq("q1"), mcq("q2")],
+        topic="ta",
+        topicLabel="TA",
+    )
+    r = run(root, "--check")  # bank drifted after the manifest was written
+    assert r.returncode == 1
+    assert "za-a.json" in r.stderr

+def write_bank_extra(root, name, subject, items, **extra):
+    bank = {"subject": subject, "topic": "t", "topicLabel": "T", "items": items}
+    bank.update(extra)
+    (root / "library" / "questions" / name).write_text(json.dumps(bank), encoding="utf-8")

+def test_item_difficulty_vocabulary(tmp_path):
+    root = copy_library(tmp_path)
+    write_bank(root, "zz-bad.json", "subj-d", [dict(mcq("q1", "t"), difficulty="brutal"), mcq("q2", "t")])
+    write_bank(root, "zz-unrated.json", "subj-d", [dict(mcq("q5", "t"), difficulty="unrated")])
+    write_bank(root, "zz-good.json", "subj-d", [dict(mcq("q3", "t"), difficulty="hard"),
+                                                dict(mcq("q4", "t"), difficulty="standard")])
+    r = run(root)
+    assert r.returncode == 1
+    assert "zz-bad.json item q1: difficulty must be easy, standard or hard (got 'brutal')" in r.stdout
+    assert "zz-unrated.json item q5: difficulty 'unrated' must be omitted (absent means unrated)" in r.stdout
+    assert "zz-good.json" not in r.stdout.split("FAIL")[-1]  # valid values raise nothing

+def test_bank_set_setorder_codes_fields(tmp_path):

+    assert "zz-bad.json: 'codes' must be IB codes like \"S1.1\", \"A1.1\" or \"2.1\"" in out
+    assert "zz-good.json: 'set'" not in out and "zz-good.json: 'codes'" not in out
